```
    30→def _run_stamp() -> str:
    31→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")

    34→def _parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    36→    if not raw:
    37→        return list(_STAGES)
    38→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    39→    for s in stages:
    40→        if s not in _STAGES:
    41→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {s!r}. Valid: {', '.join(_STAGES)}")
    42→    return stages
    43→
    44→
    45→def _ensure_triage_started(
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    services: TriageServices,
    48→) -> dict:
    49→    """Auto-start triage if not started. Returns updated plan."""
    50→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    51→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    52→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    53→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    54→        services.save_plan(plan)
    55→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started.", "cyan"))
    56→    return plan
    57→
    58→
    59→def do_run_triage_stages(
    60→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    61→    *,
    62→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Run triage stages via the selected runner."""
    65→    runner = str(getattr(args, "runner", "codex")).strip().lower()
    66→    if runner == "claude":
    67→        _run_claude_orchestrator(args, services=services)
    68→    elif runner == "codex":
    69→        _run_codex_pipeline(args, services=services)
    70→    else:

   108→
   109→
   110→def _merge_observe_outputs(
   111→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
   112→) -> str:
   113→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
   114→    parts: list[str] = []
   115→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
   116→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
   117→        content = ""
   118→        if output_file.exists():
   119→            try:
   120→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   121→            except OSError:
   122→                content = "(batch output missing)"
   123→        if not content:
   124→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
   125→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
   126→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   127→
   128→
   129→def _run_observe(
   130→    *,
   131→    si,
   132→    repo_root: Path,
   133→    prompts_dir: Path,
   134→    output_dir: Path,
   135→    logs_dir: Path,
   136→    timeout_seconds: int,
   137→    dry_run: bool = False,
   138→    append_run_log=None,
   139→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   140→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches.
   141→
   142→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success, False on failure.
   143→    """
   144→    from functools import partial
   145→
   146→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   147→
   148→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   149→
   150→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   151→
   152→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
   153→    total = len(batches)
   154→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
   155→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
   156→
   157→    # Build prompts and tasks
   158→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   159→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
   160→
   161→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
   162→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
   163→            batch_index=i + 1,
   164→            total_batches=total,
   165→            dimension_group=dims,
   166→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
   167→            repo_root=repo_root,
   168→        )
   169→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
   170→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   171→
   172→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
   173→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
   174→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
   175→
   176→        if not dry_run:
   177→            tasks[i] = partial(
   178→                run_triage_stage,
   179→                prompt=prompt,
   180→                repo_root=repo_root,
   181→                output_file=output_file,
   182→                log_file=log_file,
   183→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   184→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   185→            )
   186→
   187→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
   188→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
   189→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
   190→
   191→    if dry_run:
   192→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   193→        return True, ""
   194→
   195→    # Progress callback — same pattern as review batch execution
   196→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   197→        idx = event.batch_index
   198→        if event.event == "start":
   199→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} started", "dim"))
   200→            _log(f"observe-batch-start batch={idx + 1}")
   201→        elif event.event == "done":
   202→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   203→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   204→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   205→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   206→            _log(f"observe-batch-done batch={idx + 1} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   207→        elif event.event == "heartbeat":
   208→            details = event.details or {}
   209→            active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   210→            elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   211→            if active:
   212→                parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   213→                print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   214→
   215→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   216→        _log(f"observe-batch-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   217→
   218→    # Execute in parallel
   219→    failures = execute_batches(
   220→        tasks=tasks,
   221→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   222→        progress_fn=_progress,
   223→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   224→    )
   225→
   226→    if failures:
   227→        print(colorize(
   228→            f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   229→            "red",
   230→        ))
   231→        for idx in failures:
   232→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   233→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   234→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   235→        return False, ""
   236→
   237→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   238→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   239→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   240→    return True, merged
   241→
   242→
   243→def _run_codex_pipeline(
   244→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   245→    *,
   246→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   247→) -> None:
   248→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   249→    from .codex_runner import run_triage_stage
   250→
   251→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   252→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   253→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   254→
   255→    try:
   256→        stages_to_run = _parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
   257→    except ValueError as exc:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   259→        return
   260→
   261→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   262→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   263→    _ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
   264→
   265→    # Create run directory
   266→    stamp = _run_stamp()
   267→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   268→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   269→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   270→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   271→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   272→    for d in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   273→        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   274→
   275→    # Run log — same pattern as review batch execution
   276→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   277→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   278→    append_run_log(
   279→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   280→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   281→    )
   282→
   283→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   284→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   285→
   286→    # Collect triage input
   287→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   288→    state = runtime.state
   289→
   290→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   291→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   292→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   293→
   294→    for stage in stages_to_run:
   295→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   296→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   297→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   298→
   299→        # Skip if already confirmed (resume support)
   300→        if stage in stages and stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   301→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   302→            append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   303→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   304→            report = stages[stage].get("report", "")
   305→            if report:
   306→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   307→            continue
   308→
   309→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   310→        append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   311→
   312→        # Collect fresh triage input each stage
   313→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   314→
   315→        # Parallel observe path
   316→        used_parallel = False
   317→        if stage == "observe":
   318→            parallel_ok, merged_report = _run_observe_parallel(
   319→                si=si,
   320→                repo_root=repo_root,
   321→                prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   322→                output_dir=output_dir,
   323→                logs_dir=logs_dir,
   324→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   325→                dry_run=dry_run,
   326→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   327→            )
   328→            if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   329→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   330→                continue
   331→            if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   332→                # Record observe via the stage command with merged report
   333→                from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_observe
   334→                record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   335→                    stage="observe",
   336→                    report=merged_report,
   337→                    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   338→                )
   339→                cmd_stage_observe(record_args, services=resolved_services)
   340→                used_parallel = True
   341→            elif parallel_ok is False:
   342→                # Parallel execution failed — abort pipeline
   343→                elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   344→                print(colorize("  Observe: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   345→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage=observe elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed")
   346→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   347→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   348→                return
   349→            # parallel_ok is None → not applicable, fall through to single path
   350→
   351→        # Single-subprocess path (non-observe stages, or observe with too few issues)
   352→        if not used_parallel:
   353→            prompt = build_stage_prompt(
   354→                stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root,
   355→            )
   356→
   357→            prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   358→            safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   359→
   360→            if dry_run:
   361→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   362→                print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   363→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   364→                continue
   365→
   366→            print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   367→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   368→
   369→            output_file = output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   370→            log_file = logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   371→
   372→            exit_code = run_triage_stage(
   373→                prompt=prompt,
   374→                repo_root=repo_root,
   375→                output_file=output_file,
   376→                log_file=log_file,
   377→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   378→            )
   379→
   380→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   381→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-done stage={stage} code={exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   382→
   383→            if exit_code != 0:
   384→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {exit_code}).", "red"))
   385→                print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   386→                print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   387→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={exit_code}")
   388→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "exit_code": exit_code, "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   389→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   390→                return
   391→
   392→        # Common path: reload for validation
   393→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   394→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   395→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   396→
   397→        # Validate stage
   398→        ok, error_msg = validate_stage(stage, plan, state, repo_root, triage_input=si)
   399→        if not ok:
   400→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   401→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
   402→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   403→            append_run_log(f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}")
   404→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "validation_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed, "error": error_msg}
   405→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        # Auto-confirm via confirmation functions with generated attestation
   409→        attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, si)
   410→
   411→        # Build a synthetic args namespace for confirm functions
   412→        confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
   413→            confirm=stage,
   414→            attestation=attestation,
   415→            state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   416→        )
   417→
   418→        from ..confirmations import _cmd_confirm_stage
   419→        _cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=resolved_services)
   420→
   421→        # Re-load and check confirmation took effect
   422→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   423→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   424→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   425→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   426→        if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   427→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   428→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   429→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   430→        else:
   431→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   432→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   433→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   434→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   435→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   436→            return
   437→
   438→        # Accumulate report for next stage's prompt
   439→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   440→        if report:
   441→            prior_reports[stage] = report
   442→
   443→    if dry_run:
   444→        print(colorize("\n  [dry-run] All prompts generated. No stages executed.", "cyan"))
   445→        _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    # Auto-complete triage
   449→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   450→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   451→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   452→
   453→    # Derive strategy from all stage reports
   454→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   455→    for s in _STAGES:
   456→        report = stages_data.get(s, {}).get("report", "")
   457→        if report:
   458→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{s}] {report[:200]}")
   459→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   460→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   461→        strategy = strategy + " " + "Automated triage via codex subagent pipeline. " * 3
   462→
   463→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   464→
   465→    # Build attestation for completion
   466→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("enrich", plan, si)
   467→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   468→        complete=True,
   469→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   470→        attestation=attestation,
   471→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   472→    )
   473→
   474→    from ..stage_completion_commands import _cmd_triage_complete
   475→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=resolved_services)
   476→
   477→    total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   478→    print(colorize(f"\n  Triage pipeline complete ({total_elapsed}s).", "green"))
   479→    append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s")
   480→    _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   481→
   482→
   483→def _write_triage_run_summary(
   484→    run_dir: Path,
   485→    stamp: str,
   486→    stages: list[str],
   487→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   488→    append_run_log,
   489→) -> None:
   490→    """Write a run_summary.json with per-stage results."""
   491→    summary = {
   492→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   493→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   494→        "runner": "codex",
   495→        "stages_requested": stages,
   496→        "stage_results": stage_results,
   497→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   498→    }
   499→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   500→    safe_write_text(summary_path, json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   501→    print(colorize(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   502→    append_run_log(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = ["do_run_triage_stages"]
   506→
```

```
   102→def cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   103→    """Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → ENRICH → COMMIT."""
   104→    resolved_services = _build_triage_services()

   106→    state = runtime.state
   107→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

   111→        from .triage.runner.stage_prompts import cmd_stage_prompt
   112→        cmd_stage_prompt(args, services=resolved_services)
   113→        return
   114→    if getattr(args, "run_stages", False):
   115→        from .triage.runner import do_run_triage_stages
   116→        do_run_triage_stages(args, services=resolved_services)
   117→        return

   121→        return

   123→        _confirmations_mod.cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=resolved_services)
   124→        return

   126→        _completion_mod.cmd_triage_complete(args, services=resolved_services)
   127→        return
   128→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   129→        _completion_mod.cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=resolved_services)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   133→    if stage == "observe":
   134→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_observe(args, services=resolved_services)
   135→        return
   136→    if stage == "reflect":
   137→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=resolved_services)
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    12→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    13→    purge_ids,
    14→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    17→
    18→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    19→
    20→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]
    21→
    22→
    23→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    24→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    25→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    26→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    27→
    28→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    29→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    30→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    31→    existing = set(order)
    32→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    33→        if sid not in existing:
    34→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    35→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    36→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    37→        if sid in order:
    38→            order.remove(sid)
    39→    insert_at = 0
    40→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    41→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    42→        insert_at += 1
    43→
    44→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    45→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    46→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    47→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    48→
    49→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    51→    try:
    52→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    53→    except ValueError:
    54→        return []
    55→    cleared: list[str] = []
    56→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    57→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    60→            cleared.append(later)
    61→    return cleared
    62→
    63→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    64→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    65→    if not cleared:
    66→        return
    67→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    68→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    69→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    70→        None,
    71→    )
    72→    if next_unconfirmed:
    73→        print(colorize(
    74→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    75→            "dim",
    76→        ))
    77→
    78→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    79→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    80→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    81→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    82→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    83→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    84→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    85→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    86→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    87→
    88→def group_issues_into_observe_batches(
    89→    si,
    90→    max_batches: int = 5,
    91→) -> list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]]:
    92→    """Group issues by dimension into batches for parallel observe.
    93→
    94→    Returns list of (dimension_names, issues_subset) tuples.
    95→    Single batch if only one dimension exists.
    96→    """
    97→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    98→
    99→    if len(dim_names) <= 1:
   100→        return [(dim_names, dict(si.open_issues))]
   101→
   102→    # Distribute dimensions into balanced batches by issue count
   103→    num_batches = min(max_batches, len(dim_names))
   104→    batch_dims: list[list[str]] = [[] for _ in range(num_batches)]
   105→    batch_counts: list[int] = [0] * num_batches
   106→
   107→    # Greedy: assign each dimension (largest first) to the lightest batch
   108→    for dim in dim_names:
   109→        lightest = min(range(num_batches), key=lambda i: batch_counts[i])
   110→        batch_dims[lightest].append(dim)
   111→        batch_counts[lightest] += by_dim[dim]
   112→
   113→    # Build issue subsets per batch
   114→    # Pre-index issues by dimension
   115→    dim_to_issues: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = defaultdict(dict)
   116→    for fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
   117→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   119→        dim_to_issues[dim][fid] = f
   120→
   121→    result: list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]] = []
   122→    for dims in batch_dims:
   123→        if not dims:
   124→            continue
   125→        subset: dict[str, dict] = {}
   126→        for dim in dims:
   127→            subset.update(dim_to_issues.get(dim, {}))
   128→        if subset:
   129→            result.append((dims, subset))
   130→
   131→    return result
   132→
   133→
   134→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
   135→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
   136→    return {
   137→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   138→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   139→    }
   140→
   141→def triage_coverage(
   142→    plan: dict,
   143→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   144→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
   145→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
   146→
   147→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   148→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   149→    """
   150→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   151→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   152→    for c in clusters.values():
   153→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   154→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   155→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   156→    else:
   157→        review_ids = [
   158→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   159→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   160→        ]
   161→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   162→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   163→
   164→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   165→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   166→    return [
   167→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   168→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   169→    ]
   170→
   171→def apply_completion(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    plan: dict,
   174→    strategy: str,
   175→    *,
   176→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   177→) -> None:
   178→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   179→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   180→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   181→    state = runtime.state
   182→
   183→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(
   184→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   185→    )
   186→
   187→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   188→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   189→
   190→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   193→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   194→    open_ids = sorted(
   195→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   196→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   197→    )
   198→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_ids
   199→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   200→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   201→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   202→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   203→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   204→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   205→    if stages:
   206→        meta["last_triage"] = {
   207→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   208→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   209→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   210→        }
   211→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   212→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   213→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   214→
   215→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   216→
   217→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("issue_ids")])
   218→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   219→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   220→    if effective_strategy:
   221→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   222→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   223→
   224→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
   225→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
   226→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   227→        if fid in c.get("issue_ids", []):
   228→            return name
   229→    return None
   230→
   231→def count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   232→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
   233→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   234→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   235→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
   236→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
   237→    return dict(counts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "apply_completion",
   241→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   242→    "count_log_activity_since",
   243→    "find_cluster_for",
   244→    "group_issues_into_observe_batches",
   245→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   246→    "inject_triage_stages",
   247→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   248→    "observe_dimension_breakdown",
   249→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   250→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   251→    "purge_triage_stage",
   252→    "triage_coverage",
   253→]
   254→
```

```
     2→
```

```
     4→
     5→import subprocess

    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_process_types import CodexBatchRunnerDeps
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process import (
    12→    codex_batch_command,
    13→    run_codex_batch,
    14→)

    16→
    17→
    18→def _output_file_has_text(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when the output file exists and contains non-empty text."""
    20→    if not output_file.exists():
    21→        return False
    22→    try:
    23→        return len(output_file.read_text().strip()) > 0
    24→    except OSError:
    25→        return False
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_triage_stage(
    29→    *,
    30→    prompt: str,
    31→    repo_root: Path,
    32→    output_file: Path,
    33→    log_file: Path,
    34→    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,
    35→    validate_output_fn: Callable[[Path], bool] | None = None,
    36→) -> int:
    37→    """Execute a triage stage via codex subprocess. Returns exit code."""
    38→    deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    39→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    40→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    41→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    42→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
    43→        use_popen_runner=True,
    44→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
    45→        live_log_interval_seconds=10.0,
    46→        stall_after_output_seconds=120,
    47→        max_retries=1,
    48→        retry_backoff_seconds=5.0,
    49→        sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    50→        validate_output_fn=validate_output_fn,
    51→    )
    52→    return run_codex_batch(
    53→        prompt=prompt,
    54→        repo_root=repo_root,
    55→        output_file=output_file,
    56→        log_file=log_file,
    57→        deps=deps,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["run_triage_stage"]
    62→
```

    16→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
    17→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    18→
    19→Repo root: {repo_root}
    20→
    21→## Standards
    22→
    23→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    24→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    25→wasted. Concretely:
    26→
    27→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    28→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    29→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    30→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    31→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    32→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    33→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    34→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    35→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    36→
    37→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    38→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    39→
    40→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    41→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    42→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.

    80→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    81→
    82→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    83→
    84→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    85→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2

    88→- Count props/returns/args wrong

    90→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.

```
    23→def validate_stage(
    24→    stage: str,
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    repo_root: Path,
    28→    *,
    29→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
    30→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    31→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    34→
    35→    if stage == "observe":
    36→        if "observe" not in stages:
    37→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    38→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    39→        if len(report) < 100:
    40→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    41→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
    42→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    43→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    44→        if issue_count > 0:
    45→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    46→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
    47→                return False, (
    48→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    49→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
    50→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
    51→                )
    52→        return True, ""
    53→
    54→    if stage == "reflect":
    55→        if "reflect" not in stages:
    56→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
    57→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
    58→        if len(report) < 100:
    59→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    60→        return True, ""
    61→
    62→    if stage == "organize":
    63→        if "organize" not in stages:
    64→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
    65→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    66→        if not manual:
    67→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
    68→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    69→        if gaps:
    70→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
    71→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
    72→        unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    73→        if unclustered:
    74→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
    75→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
    76→        warnings: list[str] = []
    77→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
    78→        if overlaps:
    79→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
    80→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    81→        if scattered:
    82→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
    83→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
    84→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
    85→        if high_ratio:
    86→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
    87→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
    88→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
    89→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    90→        orphaned = [
    91→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
    92→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
    93→        ]
    94→        if orphaned:
    95→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
    96→        if warnings:
    97→            # Return success with advisory message
    98→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
    99→        return True, ""
   100→
```

```
    47→def _cmd_stage_observe(
    48→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    49→    *,
    50→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    51→) -> None:
    52→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
    53→
    54→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
    55→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
    56→    """
    57→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    58→
    59→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    60→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    61→    state = runtime.state
    62→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    63→
    64→    # Auto-start: inject triage stage IDs if not present
    65→    # NOTE: preserve existing stage data — don't clear triage_stages.
    66→    # Only _cmd_triage_start (explicit --start) should clear stages.
    67→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    68→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    69→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    70→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    71→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    72→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started (5 stages queued).", "cyan"))
    73→
    74→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    75→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    76→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    77→
    78→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
    79→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    80→    if not report:
    81→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    85→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    86→
    87→    # Edge case: 0 issues
    88→    if issue_count == 0:
    89→        cleared = _record_observe_stage(
    90→            stages,
    91→            report=report,
    92→            issue_count=0,
    93→            cited_ids=[],
    94→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    95→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    96→        )
    97→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    98→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    99→        if is_reuse:
   100→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
```

```
    31→
    32→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    33→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    34→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    35→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    36→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    37→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    38→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    39→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    40→                if ts:
    41→                    return str(ts)
    42→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    43→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    44→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    48→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    49→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    50→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    51→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    52→
    53→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    54→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    55→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    56→    """
    57→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    58→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    59→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    60→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    61→        return ""
    62→
    63→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    64→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
    65→        dim = _dimension(f)
    66→        if dim:
    67→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    68→
    69→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    70→    for fid, f in resolved_issues.items():
    71→        dim = _dimension(f)
    72→        if dim:
    73→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    74→
    75→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    76→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    77→        recurring[dim] = {
    78→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    79→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    80→        }
    81→    return recurring
    82→
    83→@dataclass
    84→class TriageDeps:
    85→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    86→
    87→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    88→
    89→def triage_epics(
    90→    plan: PlanModel,
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
    94→    dry_run: bool = False,
    95→    trigger: str = "manual",
    96→) -> TriageMutationResult:
    97→    """Run epic triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
    98→
    99→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   100→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   101→
   102→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   103→    """
   104→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   105→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   106→
   107→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   108→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   109→
   110→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   111→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   112→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   113→        return result
   114→
   115→    # Call LLM
   116→    try:
   117→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   118→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   119→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   120→        logger.error("Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   121→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   122→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   123→        return result
   124→
   125→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   126→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "ContradictionNote",
   130→    "DismissedIssue",
   131→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   132→    "TriageDeps",
   133→    "TriageInput",
   134→    "TriageMutationResult",
   135→    "TriageResult",
   136→    "apply_triage_to_plan",
   137→    "build_triage_prompt",
   138→    "collect_triage_input",
   139→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   140→    "extract_issue_citations",
   141→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   142→    "parse_triage_result",
   143→    "triage_epics",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
    18→def _make_triage_input(n_issues: int = 5) -> TriageInput:
    19→    """Create a minimal TriageInput for testing."""

    23→        issues[fid] = {

    25→            "detector": "review",
    26→            "file": f"src/foo{i}.ts",
    27→            "summary": f"Issue {i} summary",
    28→            "detail": {"dimension": f"dim_{i % 3}", "suggestion": "Fix it"},
    29→        }
    30→    return TriageInput(

    33→        existing_epics={},
    34→        dimension_scores={"dim_0": {"score": 70, "strict": 65, "failing": 2}},

    37→        previously_dismissed=[],
```

```
1→"""Attestation and confirmation handlers for plan triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    10→
    11→from .display import show_plan_summary
    12→from .helpers import (
    13→    count_log_activity_since,
    14→    observe_dimension_breakdown,
    15→    open_review_ids_from_state,
    16→    purge_triage_stage,
    17→    triage_coverage,
    18→)
    19→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    20→
    21→_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = 80
    22→
    23→
    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> str | None:
    31→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    elif stage == "enrich":
    62→        if cluster_names:
    63→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    64→            if not found:
    65→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    66→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you enriched. Mention one of: {names}"
    67→
    68→    return None
    69→
    70→def _confirm_observe(
    71→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    72→    plan: dict,
    73→    stages: dict,
    74→    attestation: str | None,
    75→    *,
    76→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    77→) -> None:
    78→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
    79→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    80→    if "observe" not in stages:
    81→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: observe stage not recorded.", "red"))
    82→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    83→        return
    84→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    85→        print(colorize("  Observe stage already confirmed.", "green"))
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    # Show summary
    89→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    90→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
    91→    obs = stages["observe"]
    92→
    93→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse issues & spot contradictions", "bold"))
    94→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
    95→
    96→    # Dimension breakdown
    97→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    98→
    99→    issue_count = obs.get("issue_count", len(si.open_issues))
   100→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
```

```
    33→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    34→from .stage_helpers import _unenriched_clusters
    35→
    36→
    37→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    38→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
    39→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    40→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    41→        if stage_name in stages:
    42→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    43→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    44→            else:
    45→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    46→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    47→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    48→        else:
    49→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
    50→
    51→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
    52→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    53→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    54→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    55→        if not manual:
    56→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    57→        elif gaps:
    58→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    59→            for name, missing in gaps:
    60→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    61→            print(colorize(
    62→                f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
    63→                "dim",
    64→            ))
    65→        else:
    66→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    67→
    68→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    69→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    70→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    71→    # Only show clusters that actually have issues (hide empty/stale ones)
    72→    active_clusters = {
    73→        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
    74→        if c.get("issue_ids")
    75→    }
    76→    if active_clusters:
    77→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    78→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    79→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    80→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    81→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    82→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    83→            tags: list[str] = []
    84→            if auto:
    85→                tags.append("auto")
    86→            if desc:
    87→                tags.append("desc")
    88→            else:
    89→                tags.append("no desc")
    90→            if steps:
    91→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    92→            else:
    93→                if not auto:
    94→                    tags.append("no steps")
    95→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    96→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
    97→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
    98→
    99→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   100→    for c in clusters.values():
   101→        all_clustered.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   102→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
   103→    if unclustered:
   104→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   105→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   106→            f = open_issues[fid]
   107→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   108→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   109→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   110→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   111→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   112→    elif open_issues:
   113→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
   114→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
   115→
   116→def print_reflect_result(
   117→    *,
   118→    issue_count: int,
   119→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   120→    recurring: dict,
   121→    report: str,
   122→    is_reuse: bool,
   123→    cleared: list,
   124→    stages: dict,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Print the reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   127→    print(colorize(
   128→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, "
   129→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   130→        "green",
   131→    ))
   132→    if is_reuse:
   133→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   134→        if cleared:
   135→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   136→    else:
   137→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   138→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   139→    if recurring_dims:
   140→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   141→            info = recurring[dim]
   142→            print(colorize(
   143→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open",
   144→                "dim",
   145→            ))
   146→
   147→    print()
   148→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) \u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   149→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   150→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
```

## Triage Step/Runner Architecture Overview

I've thoroughly explored the triage system in this codebase. Here's the complete end-to-end architecture:

### **Key Files & Directory Structure**

**Main Triage Command Handler:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage_handlers.py` — Entry point for `desloppify plan triage` command

**Triage Runner (Codex/Claude pipelines):**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/orchestrator.py` — Main orchestration logic for both "codex" and "claude" runners
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/codex_runner.py` — Subprocess execution wrapper
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts.py` — Builds prompts for each stage
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_validation.py` — Validates stage completion

**Stage Command Handlers:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_flow_commands.py` — Handles observe/reflect/organize stage recording
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_completion_commands.py` — Handles enrich & complete (commit)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/confirmations.py` — Attestation and stage confirmation logic

**Supporting Infrastructure:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/helpers.py` — Utilities (issue batching, coverage tracking, cascading clears)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/services.py` — Dependency injection bundle (TriageServices)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display.py` — Dashboard rendering
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/__init__.py` — Runner module exports

**Engine Layer:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_triage.py` — Core triage engine API
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_triage_prompt.py` — Prompt templates & TriageInput collection
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_triage_parsing.py` — Parse LLM responses
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_triage_apply.py` — Apply triage results to plan

**Schema & Queue Management:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` — Defines `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `TRIAGE_IDS`, stage dependency graph
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage_playbook.py` — Canonical CLI commands, stage labels, dependency metadata

---

### **The 5-Stage Triage Pipeline**

Triage orchestrates a **sequential 5-stage workflow** for resolving review/concerns issues:

1. **OBSERVE** (`triage::observe`)
   - Verify each open review issue against actual source code
   - Catch false positives and exaggerated claims
   - Use **parallel subagents** (one per dimension group) to investigate concurrently
   - Must cite specific issue IDs (10% of issues or min 5)
   - Output: Analysis report with verdicts

2. **REFLECT** (`triage::reflect`)
   - Filter genuine issues from observe verdicts
   - Identify themes and root causes
   - Design strategy (NOT a restatement of observe)
   - Output: Strategic briefing document

3. **ORGANIZE** (`triage::organize`)
   - Create manual clusters (groups of related issues)
   - Map issues to clusters based on file/directory overlap
   - Assign priorities
   - Output: Cluster definitions with descriptions

4. **ENRICH** (`triage::enrich`)
   - Add actionable steps to each cluster
   - Specify file paths, dependencies, and effort estimates
   - Reference issues per step
   - Use **parallel subagents** (one per cluster) for scalability
   - Output: Executor-ready plan

5. **COMMIT** (`triage::commit`) — *implicit completion*
   - Final confirmation of entire strategy
   - Purges all triage stage IDs from queue
   - Archives stage reports in `epic_triage_meta.last_triage`
   - Output: Ready-to-execute plan

---

### **Two Runner Modes**

**1. Codex Runner** (`--runner codex`, default)
- Fully automated pipeline
- Spawns codex subprocesses for each stage (respects `--only-stages`)
- Parallel observe: splits issues by dimension into concurrent batches
- Auto-validates each stage; stops on failure (resumable)
- Auto-attests stages using generated attestations
- Auto-completes triage at the end
- Output: `/home/.desloppify/triage_runs/{timestamp}/` with logs, prompts, and summaries

**2. Claude Orchestrator** (`--runner claude`)
- Manual, human-in-the-loop mode
- Prints instructions for launching subagents (Agent tool)
- User manually runs `desloppify plan triage --stage-prompt <stage>` between stages
- User launches separate subagents per stage (or sub-subagents for observe/enrich)
- User confirms each stage with `desloppify plan triage --confirm <stage> --attestation "..."`
- Final completion: `desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..." --attestation "..."`

---

### **End-to-End Flow (Codex Pipeline)**

```
1. --run-stages → _run_codex_pipeline()
   ↓
2. Load plan, auto-start if needed (inject TRIAGE_STAGE_IDs)
   ↓
3. For each stage in ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]:
   a. Skip if already confirmed (resume support)
   b. Collect fresh triage input (open issues, clusters, etc.)
   c. Build stage-specific prompt (includes prior stage reports)
   d. If OBSERVE && multiple issues: run parallel batches (by dimension)
      Otherwise: run single subprocess
   e. Validate stage output (report length, citations, cluster state, etc.)
   f. Auto-attest using build_auto_attestation()
   g. Record confirmation via _cmd_confirm_stage()
   h. Accumulate report for next stage's prompt
   ↓
4. Auto-complete triage:
   - Derive strategy from all stage reports
   - Call _cmd_triage_complete() with auto-generated attestation
   - Purges all triage::* IDs from queue
   - Saves plan
   ↓
5. Write run_summary.json with per-stage results and timing
```

---

### **Key Data Structures & Concepts**

**Triage Queue IDs:**
```python
TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = ("triage::observe", "triage::reflect", "triage::organize", "triage::enrich", "triage::commit")
TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
```
These are synthetic queue items (not real issues) that mark triage progress.

**Stage Dependencies:**
```python
TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES = {
    "observe": set(),
    "reflect": {"observe"},
    "organize": {"reflect"},
    "enrich": {"organize"},
    "commit": {"enrich"},
}
```
Earlier stages must be confirmed before later ones.

**Plan Storage:**
```python
plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {
    "triage_stages": {
        "observe": {"report": "...", "cited_ids": [...], "issue_count": N, "confirmed_at": "..."},
        "reflect": {"report": "...", "confirmed_at": "..."},
        "organize": {"report": "...", "confirmed_at": "..."},
        "enrich": {"report": "...", "confirmed_at": "..."},
    },
    "last_triage": {  # archived after completion
        "completed_at": "...",
        "stages": {...},
        "strategy": "..."
    },
    "strategy_summary": "...",
    "issue_snapshot_hash": "...",  # detect when new issues arrive
    "triaged_ids": [...]  # which issues were included
}
```

**TriageServices** (dependency injection):
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TriageServices:
    command_runtime: Callable
    load_plan: Callable
    save_plan: Callable
    collect_triage_input: Callable
    detect_recurring_patterns: Callable
    append_log_entry: Callable
    extract_issue_citations: Callable
    build_triage_prompt: Callable
```
All triage commands receive this bundle, enabling test mocking.

---

### **Validation & Guardrails**

**Observe validation:**
- Report ≥100 chars
- Citations ≥ min(5, 10% of issues)

**Reflect validation:**
- Report ≥100 chars

**Organize validation:**
- ≥1 manual cluster with issues
- All clusters enriched (description, steps)
- All review issues in a cluster
- Advisory warnings (file overlaps, scattered dirs, 1:1 step:issue ratio)

**Enrich validation:**
- File paths exist on disk
- All steps have effort tags
- No shallow/vague step details
- Issue references valid

**Completion validation:**
- Organize stage confirmed
- Enrich stage confirmed
- ≥1 cluster with issues
- All clusters enriched

---

### **Parallel Observe Batching**

For large codebases, observe splits issues by dimension:
- Groups issues by `detail.dimension`
- Distributes dimensions into ≤5 batches (greedy load-balancing)
- Spawns parallel codex subprocesses per batch
- Each batch: one subagent per dimension group
- Merges outputs into single observe report with dimension headers
- Resilient: heartbeat monitoring, live log streaming, per-batch failure tracking

---

### **CLI Integration**

**From parser_groups_plan_impl.py:**
```
desloppify plan triage --run-stages [--runner codex|claude] [--only-stages observe,reflect] [--stage-timeout-seconds 1800] [--dry-run]
desloppify plan triage --stage {observe|reflect|organize|enrich} --report "..."
desloppify plan triage --confirm {observe|reflect|organize|enrich} --attestation "..."
desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..." [--attestation "..."]
desloppify plan triage --stage-prompt {observe|reflect|organize|enrich}  # for orchestrator
desloppify plan triage --start  # manual restart
desloppify plan triage  # show dashboard
```

### **Test Coverage**

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/plan/test_triage_runner.py` — Stage prompts, validation, orchestration
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/plan/test_triage_*.py` — Feature tests (auto-start, coverage, attestation, confirmation, dependency guard, fold-confirm, jump-back, logging, tooling fixes)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_epic_triage.py` — Engine-layer triage logic

---

### **Summary**

The triage runner is a **sophisticated orchestration system** that:
- Automates a human-curated 5-stage planning workflow
- Supports both fully-automated (codex) and human-in-the-loop (claude) execution
- Uses parallel subagents to scale (observe batches, enrich clusters)
- Validates output at each stage with strict guardrails
- Stores incremental stage progress in `plan.json`, enabling resumption
- Integrates tightly with the queue/cluster model for executable plans

> DEVELOPER

I'm working on a triage pipeline for a codebase health tool (desloppify). The pipeline has stages: observe → reflect → organize → enrich → commit. We want to add a "sense-check" stage between enrich and commit.

The sense-check stage needs to be extremely robust — it should catch every possible failure mode before the plan gets handed to an executor (a "code monkey" who will follow instructions literally).

The user wants multiple specialized subagents, not just one. Here's what they suggested:
1. One that verifies claims against actual code (are file paths real? do the line numbers match? is the described code still there?)
2. One that checks sequencing/dependency ordering
3. One that checks if steps are truly "code monkey proof" (could someone with zero context execute them?)

I need you to think deeply about this and design the subagent architecture. Consider:

**What failure modes exist after enrich?**
- Steps reference files/lines that don't exist or have changed
- Steps describe code patterns that aren't actually there (hallucinated context)
- Steps are too vague ("refactor this", "clean up that")
- Steps within a cluster are in wrong order (step 3 depends on step 1's output but step 2 is between them)
- Clusters are in wrong dependency order
- Two steps in different clusters modify the same file but clusters have no dependency
- Effort tags are wrong (trivial for something complex, or large for something simple)
- Steps reference issues that were already resolved or skipped
- Duplicate work across clusters (two steps doing essentially the same thing)
- Missing steps (an issue is referenced but the step doesn't actually address it)
- Steps that would break other code (e.g., renaming an export without updating importers)
- Over-specified steps that are actually wrong (specific line numbers that are off by a few)

**Design constraints:**
- Each subagent runs as a codex subprocess (same as observe/enrich batches)
- Subagents can read the codebase and run desloppify CLI commands
- The sense-check stage should be able to actually FIX problems it finds (update steps, reorder, add detail) — not just report them
- Must work with the existing validation infrastructure
- Should be parallelizable where possible

**Questions to answer:**
1. What subagents should we have? (Name, responsibility, what it reads, what it can fix)
2. Which can run in parallel vs which need to be sequential?
3. What does the merged output look like?
4. What validation gates should block confirmation?
5. Should there be a "retry" mechanism if a subagent finds too many problems?

Think about this like you're designing a code review system where the reviewers are specialized and thorough. What would make this bulletproof?

> AGENT

I'll start by understanding the existing pipeline architecture, the triage runner, and the plan/cluster data structures to design this properly.

```
    46→def _cmd_triage_start(
    47→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    48→    *,
    49→    services: _services_mod.TriageServices | None = None,
    50→) -> None:
    51→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    52→    resolved_services = services or _build_triage_services()
    53→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    54→
    55→    if _helpers_mod.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    56→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    57→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    58→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    59→        if stages:
    60→            print(
    61→                colorize(
    62→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.", "yellow"
    63→                )
    64→            )
    65→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    66→            _helpers_mod.inject_triage_stages(plan)
    67→            resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    68→            resolved_services.append_log_entry(
    69→                plan,
    70→                "triage_start",
    71→                actor="user",
    72→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    73→            )
    74→            resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    75→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    76→        else:
    77→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    78→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    79→        return
    80→

    82→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})

    95→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
    96→    print(colorize("  Planning mode started (5 stages queued).", "green"))
    97→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    98→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
    99→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))

   101→
   102→def cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   103→    """Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → ENRICH → COMMIT."""
   104→    resolved_services = _build_triage_services()
   105→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   106→    state = runtime.state
   107→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    if getattr(args, "stage_prompt", None):
   111→        from .triage.runner.stage_prompts import cmd_stage_prompt
   112→        cmd_stage_prompt(args, services=resolved_services)
   113→        return
   114→    if getattr(args, "run_stages", False):
   115→        from .triage.runner import do_run_triage_stages
   116→        do_run_triage_stages(args, services=resolved_services)
   117→        return
   118→
   119→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   120→        _cmd_triage_start(args, services=resolved_services)
   121→        return
   122→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   123→        _confirmations_mod.cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=resolved_services)
   124→        return
   125→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   126→        _completion_mod.cmd_triage_complete(args, services=resolved_services)
   127→        return
   128→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   129→        _completion_mod.cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=resolved_services)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   133→    if stage == "observe":
   134→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_observe(args, services=resolved_services)
   135→        return
   136→    if stage == "reflect":
   137→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=resolved_services)
   138→        return
   139→    if stage == "organize":
   140→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_organize(args, services=resolved_services)
   141→        return
   142→    if stage == "enrich":
   143→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_enrich(args, services=resolved_services)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   147→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   148→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   149→        prompt = resolved_services.build_triage_prompt(si)
   150→        print(colorize("  Epic triage — dry run", "bold"))
   151→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   152→        print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   153→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   154→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   155→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   156→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   157→        print()
   158→        print(prompt)
   159→        return
   160→
   161→    _display_mod.cmd_triage_dashboard(args, services=resolved_services)
   162→

   164→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   165→    "_triage_coverage",
```
