1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, NotRequired, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
     8→    upgrade_plan_to_v8 as _upgrade_plan_to_v8,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import VALID_SKIP_KINDS
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→PLAN_VERSION = 8
    14→
    15→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    16→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    17→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    18→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    23→    issue_id: Required[str]
    24→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    25→    reason: str | None
    26→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    27→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    28→    created_at: str
    29→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    30→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    31→
    32→
    33→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    34→    issue_id: Required[str]
    35→    description: str | None
    36→    note: str | None
    37→    cluster: str | None
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class ActionStep(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    title: Required[str]        # Short summary, 1 line
    44→    detail: str                 # Long description, paragraphs OK
    45→    issue_refs: list[str]       # Issue ID suffixes this step addresses
    46→    done: bool                  # Completion tracking (default False)
    47→
    48→
    49→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    50→    name: Required[str]
    51→    description: str | None
    52→    issue_ids: list[str]
    53→    created_at: str
    54→    updated_at: str
    55→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    56→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    57→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    58→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    59→    optional: bool
    60→    thesis: str
    61→    direction: str
    62→    root_cause: str
    63→    supersedes: list[str]
    64→    dismissed: list[str]
    65→    agent_safe: bool
    66→    dependency_order: int
    67→    action_steps: list[str | ActionStep]
    68→    priority: int
    69→    source_clusters: list[str]
    70→    status: str
    71→    triage_version: int
    72→
    73→
    74→class CommitRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    75→    sha: Required[str]           # git commit SHA
    76→    branch: str | None           # branch name
    77→    issue_ids: list[str]       # issues included
    78→    recorded_at: str             # ISO timestamp
    79→    note: str | None             # user-provided rationale
    80→    cluster_name: str | None     # cluster context
    81→
    82→
    83→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    84→    timestamp: Required[str]
    85→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    86→    issue_ids: list[str]
    87→    cluster_name: str | None
    88→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    89→    note: str | None
    90→    detail: dict[str, Any]  # action-specific extra data
    91→
    92→
    93→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    94→    original_id: Required[str]
    95→    original_detector: str
    96→    original_file: str
    97→    original_summary: str
    98→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    99→    superseded_at: str
   100→    remapped_to: str | None
   101→    candidates: list[str]
   102→    note: str | None
   103→
   104→
   105→class PlanStartScores(TypedDict, total=False):
   106→    """Frozen score snapshot captured when a plan cycle starts."""
   107→
   108→    strict: float
   109→    overall: float
   110→    objective: float
   111→    verified: float
   112→    reset: bool
   113→
   114→
   115→class TriageStagePayload(TypedDict, total=False):
   116→    """Persisted payload for one triage stage checkpoint."""
   117→
   118→    stage: str
   119→    report: str
   120→    cited_ids: list[str]
   121→    timestamp: str
   122→    issue_count: int
   123→    recurring_dims: list[str]
   124→    confirmed_at: str
   125→    confirmed_text: str
   126→
   127→
   128→class LastTriageSnapshot(TypedDict, total=False):
   129→    """Archived triage stage state captured when triage is completed."""
   130→
   131→    completed_at: str
   132→    stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   133→    strategy: str
   134→
   135→
   136→class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
   137→    """Metadata persisted for the multi-stage triage flow."""
   138→
   139→    triaged_ids: list[str]
   140→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
   141→    issue_snapshot_hash: str
   142→    strategy_summary: str
   143→    trigger: str
   144→    version: int
   145→    last_run: str
   146→    last_completed_at: str
   147→    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   148→    stage_snapshot_hash: str
   149→    stage_refresh_required: bool
   150→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   151→
   152→
   153→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   154→    version: Required[int]
   155→    created: Required[str]
   156→    updated: Required[str]
   157→    queue_order: list[str]
   158→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   159→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   160→    active_cluster: str | None
   161→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   162→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   163→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   164→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   165→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   166→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   167→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   168→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   169→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   170→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   171→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   172→
   173→
   174→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   175→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    return {
   178→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   179→        "created": now,
   180→        "updated": now,
   181→        "queue_order": [],
   182→        "deferred": [],
   183→        "skipped": {},
   184→        "active_cluster": None,
   185→        "overrides": {},
   186→        "clusters": {},
   187→        "superseded": {},
   188→        "promoted_ids": [],
   189→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   190→        "execution_log": [],
   191→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   192→        "commit_log": [],
   193→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   194→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   195→    }
   196→
   197→
   198→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   199→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   200→
   201→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   202→    """
   203→    defaults = empty_plan()
   204→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   205→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   206→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   207→
   208→
   209→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   210→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   211→    return {
   212→        name: cluster
   213→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   214→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   219→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   220→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   221→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   222→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   223→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   224→
   225→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   226→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   227→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   228→    if overlap:
   229→        raise ValueError(
   230→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   231→        )
   232→
   233→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   234→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   235→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   236→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   237→            raise ValueError(
   238→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   239→            )
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ActionStep",
   244→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   245→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   246→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   247→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   248→    "Cluster",
   249→    "CommitRecord",
   250→    "ItemOverride",
   251→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   252→    "PlanModel",
   253→    "PlanStartScores",
   254→    "SkipEntry",
   255→    "SupersededEntry",
   256→    "TriageStagePayload",
   257→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   258→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   259→    "empty_plan",
   260→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   261→    "triage_clusters",
   262→    "validate_plan",
   263→]
   264→

    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→
    18→from ..helpers import (
    19→    group_issues_into_observe_batches,
    20→    has_triage_in_queue,
    21→    inject_triage_stages,
    22→)
    23→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    24→from .stage_prompts import build_observe_batch_prompt, build_stage_prompt
    25→from .stage_validation import build_auto_attestation, validate_stage
    26→
    27→_STAGES = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich")
    28→
    29→
    30→def _run_stamp() -> str:
    31→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    32→
    33→
    34→def _parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    36→    if not raw:
    37→        return list(_STAGES)
    38→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    39→    for s in stages:
    40→        if s not in _STAGES:
    41→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {s!r}. Valid: {', '.join(_STAGES)}")
    42→    return stages
    43→
    44→
    45→def _ensure_triage_started(
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    services: TriageServices,
    48→) -> dict:
    49→    """Auto-start triage if not started. Returns updated plan."""
    50→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    51→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    52→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    53→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    54→        services.save_plan(plan)
    55→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started.", "cyan"))
    56→    return plan
    57→
    58→
    59→def do_run_triage_stages(
    60→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    61→    *,
    62→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Run triage stages via the selected runner."""
    65→    runner = str(getattr(args, "runner", "codex")).strip().lower()
    66→    if runner == "claude":
    67→        _run_claude_orchestrator(args, services=services)
    68→    elif runner == "codex":
    69→        _run_codex_pipeline(args, services=services)
    70→    else:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  Unknown runner: {runner}. Use 'codex' or 'claude'.", "red"))
    72→
    73→
    74→def _run_claude_orchestrator(
    75→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    76→    *,
    77→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    78→) -> None:
    79→    """Print orchestrator instructions for Claude Code agent."""
    80→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    81→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    82→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    83→    _ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
    84→
    85→    print(colorize("\n  Claude triage orchestrator mode.", "bold"))
    86→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    87→    print(colorize(
    88→        "  You are the orchestrator. For each stage, launch a subagent.\n",
    89→        "cyan",
    90→    ))
    91→    print("  For each stage (observe → reflect → organize → enrich):\n")
    92→    print("    1. Get the prompt:")
    93→    print("       desloppify plan triage --stage-prompt <stage>\n")
    94→    print("    2. Launch a subagent (Agent tool) with that prompt.\n")
    95→    print("    3. Verify the stage was recorded:")
    96→    print("       desloppify plan triage\n")
    97→    print("    4. Confirm:")
    98→    print('       desloppify plan triage --confirm <stage> --attestation "..."\n')
    99→    print("    5. Proceed to the next stage.\n")
   100→    print("  After all 4 stages:")
   101→    print('    desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..." --attestation "..."\n')
   102→    print(colorize("  Key rules:", "yellow"))
   103→    print("    - ONE subagent per stage. Don't combine stages.")
   104→    print("    - Check the dashboard between stages.")
   105→    print("    - Observe subagent should use sub-subagents (one per dimension group).")
   106→    print("    - Enrich subagent should use sub-subagents (one per cluster).")
   107→    print("    - If a stage fails validation, fix and re-record.")
   108→
   109→
   110→def _merge_observe_outputs(
   111→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
   112→) -> str:
   113→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
   114→    parts: list[str] = []
   115→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
   116→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
   117→        content = ""
   118→        if output_file.exists():
   119→            try:
   120→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   121→            except OSError:
   122→                content = "(batch output missing)"
   123→        if not content:
   124→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
   125→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
   126→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   127→
   128→
   129→def _run_observe(
   130→    *,
   131→    si,
   132→    repo_root: Path,
   133→    prompts_dir: Path,
   134→    output_dir: Path,
   135→    logs_dir: Path,
   136→    timeout_seconds: int,
   137→    dry_run: bool = False,
   138→    append_run_log=None,
   139→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   140→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches.
   141→
   142→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success, False on failure.
   143→    """
   144→    from functools import partial
   145→
   146→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   147→
   148→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   149→
   150→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   151→
   152→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
   153→    total = len(batches)
   154→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
   155→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
   156→
   157→    # Build prompts and tasks
   158→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   159→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
   160→
   161→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
   162→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
   163→            batch_index=i + 1,
   164→            total_batches=total,
   165→            dimension_group=dims,
   166→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
   167→            repo_root=repo_root,
   168→        )
   169→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
   170→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   171→
   172→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
   173→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
   174→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
   175→
   176→        if not dry_run:
   177→            tasks[i] = partial(
   178→                run_triage_stage,
   179→                prompt=prompt,
   180→                repo_root=repo_root,
   181→                output_file=output_file,
   182→                log_file=log_file,
   183→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   184→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   185→            )
   186→
   187→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
   188→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
   189→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
   190→
   191→    if dry_run:
   192→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   193→        return True, ""
   194→
   195→    # Progress callback — same pattern as review batch execution
   196→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   197→        idx = event.batch_index
   198→        if event.event == "start":
   199→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} started", "dim"))
   200→            _log(f"observe-batch-start batch={idx + 1}")
   201→        elif event.event == "done":
   202→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   203→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   204→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   205→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   206→            _log(f"observe-batch-done batch={idx + 1} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   207→        elif event.event == "heartbeat":
   208→            details = event.details or {}
   209→            active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   210→            elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   211→            if active:
   212→                parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   213→                print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   214→
   215→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   216→        _log(f"observe-batch-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   217→
   218→    # Execute in parallel
   219→    failures = execute_batches(
   220→        tasks=tasks,
   221→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   222→        progress_fn=_progress,
   223→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   224→    )
   225→
   226→    if failures:
   227→        print(colorize(
   228→            f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   229→            "red",
   230→        ))
   231→        for idx in failures:
   232→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   233→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   234→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   235→        return False, ""
   236→
   237→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   238→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   239→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   240→    return True, merged
   241→
   242→
   243→def _run_codex_pipeline(
   244→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   245→    *,
   246→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   247→) -> None:
   248→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   249→    from .codex_runner import run_triage_stage
   250→
   251→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   252→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   253→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   254→
   255→    try:
   256→        stages_to_run = _parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
   257→    except ValueError as exc:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   259→        return
   260→
   261→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   262→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   263→    _ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
   264→
   265→    # Create run directory
   266→    stamp = _run_stamp()
   267→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   268→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   269→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   270→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   271→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   272→    for d in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   273→        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   274→
   275→    # Run log — same pattern as review batch execution
   276→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   277→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   278→    append_run_log(
   279→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   280→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   281→    )
   282→
   283→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   284→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   285→
   286→    # Collect triage input
   287→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   288→    state = runtime.state
   289→
   290→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   291→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   292→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   293→
   294→    for stage in stages_to_run:
   295→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   296→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   297→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   298→
   299→        # Skip if already confirmed (resume support)
   300→        if stage in stages and stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   301→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   302→            append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   303→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   304→            report = stages[stage].get("report", "")
   305→            if report:
   306→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   307→            continue
   308→
   309→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   310→        append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   311→
   312→        # Collect fresh triage input each stage
   313→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   314→
   315→        # Parallel observe path
   316→        used_parallel = False
   317→        if stage == "observe":
   318→            parallel_ok, merged_report = _run_observe_parallel(
   319→                si=si,
   320→                repo_root=repo_root,
   321→                prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   322→                output_dir=output_dir,
   323→                logs_dir=logs_dir,
   324→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   325→                dry_run=dry_run,
   326→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   327→            )
   328→            if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   329→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   330→                continue
   331→            if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   332→                # Record observe via the stage command with merged report
   333→                from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_observe
   334→                record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   335→                    stage="observe",
   336→                    report=merged_report,
   337→                    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   338→                )
   339→                cmd_stage_observe(record_args, services=resolved_services)
   340→                used_parallel = True
   341→            elif parallel_ok is False:
   342→                # Parallel execution failed — abort pipeline
   343→                elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   344→                print(colorize("  Observe: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   345→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage=observe elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed")
   346→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   347→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   348→                return
   349→            # parallel_ok is None → not applicable, fall through to single path
   350→
   351→        # Single-subprocess path (non-observe stages, or observe with too few issues)
   352→        if not used_parallel:
   353→            prompt = build_stage_prompt(
   354→                stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root,
   355→            )
   356→
   357→            prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   358→            safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   359→
   360→            if dry_run:
   361→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   362→                print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   363→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   364→                continue
   365→
   366→            print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   367→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   368→
   369→            output_file = output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   370→            log_file = logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   371→
   372→            exit_code = run_triage_stage(
   373→                prompt=prompt,
   374→                repo_root=repo_root,
   375→                output_file=output_file,
   376→                log_file=log_file,
   377→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   378→            )
   379→
   380→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   381→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-done stage={stage} code={exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   382→
   383→            if exit_code != 0:
   384→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {exit_code}).", "red"))
   385→                print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   386→                print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   387→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={exit_code}")
   388→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "exit_code": exit_code, "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   389→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   390→                return
   391→
   392→        # Common path: reload for validation
   393→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   394→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   395→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   396→
   397→        # Validate stage
   398→        ok, error_msg = validate_stage(stage, plan, state, repo_root, triage_input=si)
   399→        if not ok:
   400→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   401→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
   402→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   403→            append_run_log(f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}")
   404→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "validation_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed, "error": error_msg}
   405→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        # Auto-confirm via confirmation functions with generated attestation
   409→        attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, si)
   410→
   411→        # Build a synthetic args namespace for confirm functions
   412→        confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
   413→            confirm=stage,
   414→            attestation=attestation,
   415→            state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   416→        )
   417→
   418→        from ..confirmations import _cmd_confirm_stage
   419→        _cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=resolved_services)
   420→
   421→        # Re-load and check confirmation took effect
   422→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   423→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   424→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   425→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   426→        if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   427→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   428→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   429→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   430→        else:
   431→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   432→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   433→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   434→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   435→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   436→            return
   437→
   438→        # Accumulate report for next stage's prompt
   439→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   440→        if report:
   441→            prior_reports[stage] = report
   442→
   443→    if dry_run:
   444→        print(colorize("\n  [dry-run] All prompts generated. No stages executed.", "cyan"))
   445→        _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    # Auto-complete triage
   449→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   450→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   451→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   452→
   453→    # Derive strategy from all stage reports
   454→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   455→    for s in _STAGES:
   456→        report = stages_data.get(s, {}).get("report", "")
   457→        if report:
   458→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{s}] {report[:200]}")
   459→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   460→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   461→        strategy = strategy + " " + "Automated triage via codex subagent pipeline. " * 3
   462→
   463→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   464→
   465→    # Build attestation for completion
   466→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("enrich", plan, si)
   467→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   468→        complete=True,
   469→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   470→        attestation=attestation,
   471→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   472→    )
   473→
   474→    from ..stage_completion_commands import _cmd_triage_complete
   475→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=resolved_services)
   476→
   477→    total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   478→    print(colorize(f"\n  Triage pipeline complete ({total_elapsed}s).", "green"))
   479→    append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s")
   480→    _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   481→
   482→
   483→def _write_triage_run_summary(
   484→    run_dir: Path,
   485→    stamp: str,
   486→    stages: list[str],
   487→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   488→    append_run_log,
   489→) -> None:
   490→    """Write a run_summary.json with per-stage results."""
   491→    summary = {
   492→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   493→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   494→        "runner": "codex",
   495→        "stages_requested": stages,
   496→        "stage_results": stage_results,
   497→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   498→    }
   499→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   500→    safe_write_text(summary_path, json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   501→    print(colorize(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   502→    append_run_log(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = ["do_run_triage_stages"]
   506→

1→"""Thin wrapper around review runner_process for triage stage execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import subprocess
     6→import time
     7→from collections.abc import Callable
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_process_types import CodexBatchRunnerDeps
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_process import (
    12→    codex_batch_command,
    13→    run_codex_batch,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    16→
    17→
    18→def _output_file_has_text(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when the output file exists and contains non-empty text."""
    20→    if not output_file.exists():
    21→        return False
    22→    try:
    23→        return len(output_file.read_text().strip()) > 0
    24→    except OSError:
    25→        return False
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_triage_stage(
    29→    *,
    30→    prompt: str,
    31→    repo_root: Path,
    32→    output_file: Path,
    33→    log_file: Path,
    34→    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,
    35→    validate_output_fn: Callable[[Path], bool] | None = None,
    36→) -> int:
    37→    """Execute a triage stage via codex subprocess. Returns exit code."""
    38→    deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    39→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    40→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    41→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    42→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
    43→        use_popen_runner=True,
    44→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
    45→        live_log_interval_seconds=10.0,
    46→        stall_after_output_seconds=120,
    47→        max_retries=1,
    48→        retry_backoff_seconds=5.0,
    49→        sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    50→        validate_output_fn=validate_output_fn,
    51→    )
    52→    return run_codex_batch(
    53→        prompt=prompt,
    54→        repo_root=repo_root,
    55→        output_file=output_file,
    56→        log_file=log_file,
    57→        deps=deps,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["run_triage_stage"]
    62→
```

1→"""Post-subagent validation and auto-attestation for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage_prompt import TriageInput
     8→
     9→from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues, observe_dimension_breakdown
    10→from ..stage_helpers import _unclustered_review_issues, _unenriched_clusters
    11→from .._stage_validation import (
    12→    _cluster_file_overlaps,
    13→    _clusters_with_directory_scatter,
    14→    _clusters_with_high_step_ratio,
    15→    _shallow_steps,
    16→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    17→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    18→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    19→    _steps_without_effort,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def validate_stage(
    24→    stage: str,
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    repo_root: Path,
    28→    *,
    29→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
    30→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    31→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    34→
    35→    if stage == "observe":
    36→        if "observe" not in stages:
    37→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    38→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    39→        if len(report) < 100:
    40→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    41→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
    42→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    43→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    44→        if issue_count > 0:
    45→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    46→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
    47→                return False, (
    48→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    49→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
    50→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
    51→                )
    52→        return True, ""
    53→
    54→    if stage == "reflect":
    55→        if "reflect" not in stages:
    56→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
    57→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
    58→        if len(report) < 100:
    59→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    60→        return True, ""
    61→
    62→    if stage == "organize":
    63→        if "organize" not in stages:
    64→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
    65→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    66→        if not manual:
    67→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
    68→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    69→        if gaps:
    70→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
    71→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
    72→        unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    73→        if unclustered:
    74→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
    75→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
    76→        warnings: list[str] = []
    77→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
    78→        if overlaps:
    79→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
    80→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    81→        if scattered:
    82→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
    83→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
    84→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
    85→        if high_ratio:
    86→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
    87→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
    88→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
    89→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    90→        orphaned = [
    91→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
    92→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
    93→        ]
    94→        if orphaned:
    95→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
    96→        if warnings:
    97→            # Return success with advisory message
    98→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
    99→        return True, ""
   100→
   101→    if stage == "enrich":
   102→        if "enrich" not in stages:
   103→            return False, "Enrich stage not recorded."
   104→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   105→        if shallow:
   106→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   107→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs."
   108→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   109→        if bad_paths:
   110→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   111→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
   112→        # Effort tags are now blocking
   113→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   114→        if untagged:
   115→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   116→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)."
   117→        # Issue refs are now blocking
   118→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   119→        if no_refs:
   120→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   121→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
   122→        # Vague detail is now blocking
   123→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   124→        if vague:
   125→            return False, (
   126→                f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   127→                f"Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   128→            )
   129→        return True, ""
   130→
   131→    return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   132→
   133→
   134→def validate_completion(
   135→    plan: dict,
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    repo_root: Path,
   138→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   139→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   140→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   141→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   142→
   143→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"):
   144→        if required not in stages:
   145→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   146→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   147→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   148→
   149→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   150→    if not manual:
   151→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   152→
   153→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   154→    if gaps:
   155→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   156→
   157→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   158→    if unclustered:
   159→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   160→
   161→    # Quality warnings (non-blocking but logged)
   162→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   163→    # Check dependency ordering
   164→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   165→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   166→        if name in deps:
   167→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   168→
   169→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   170→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   171→    total_steps = 0
   172→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   173→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   174→            continue
   175→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   176→        total_steps += len(steps)
   177→        if steps and all(
   178→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   179→        ):
   180→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   181→
   182→    return True, ""
   183→
   184→
   185→def build_auto_attestation(
   186→    stage: str,
   187→    plan: dict,
   188→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   189→) -> str:
   190→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   191→    if stage == "observe":
   192→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   193→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   194→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   195→        return (
   196→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   197→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   198→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   199→        )
   200→
   201→    if stage == "reflect":
   202→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   203→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   204→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   205→        return (
   206→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   207→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   208→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   209→        )
   210→
   211→    if stage == "organize":
   212→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   213→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   214→        return (
   215→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   216→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   217→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   218→        )
   219→
   220→    if stage == "enrich":
   221→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   222→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   223→        return (
   224→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   225→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   226→            f"against the actual codebase."
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   230→
   231→
   232→__all__ = [
   233→    "build_auto_attestation",
   234→    "validate_completion",
   235→    "validate_stage",
   236→]
   237→

1→"""Per-stage subagent prompt builders for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage_prompt import TriageInput, build_triage_prompt
    10→
    11→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    12→
    13→_STAGES = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich")
    14→
    15→_PREAMBLE = """\
    16→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
    17→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    18→
    19→Repo root: {repo_root}
    20→
    21→## Standards
    22→
    23→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    24→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    25→wasted. Concretely:
    26→
    27→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    28→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    29→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    30→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    31→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    32→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    33→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    34→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    35→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    36→
    37→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    38→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    39→
    40→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    41→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    42→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.
    43→"""
    44→
    45→_CLI_REFERENCE = """\
    46→## CLI Command Reference
    47→
    48→### Stage recording
    49→```
    50→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<analysis>"
    51→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<strategy>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    52→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    53→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars>"
    54→```
    55→
    56→### Cluster management
    57→```
    58→desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "<what this cluster addresses>"
    59→desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns...>
    60→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "<desc>" --steps "step 1" "step 2"
    61→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "<title>" --detail "<sub-points>" --effort small --issue-refs <id1> <id2>
    62→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "<sub-points>" --effort medium --issue-refs <id1>
    63→desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster-name>
    64→desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    65→desloppify plan cluster list --verbose
    66→```
    67→
    68→### Skip/dismiss
    69→```
    70→desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason>" --attest "<attestation>"
    71→```
    72→
    73→### Effort tags
    74→Valid values: trivial, small, medium, large. Set on steps via --effort flag.
    75→"""
    76→
    77→
    78→def _observe_instructions() -> str:
    79→    return """\
    80→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    81→
    82→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    83→
    84→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    85→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    86→- Describe code that was already refactored
    87→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    88→- Count props/returns/args wrong
    89→
    90→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    91→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    92→
    93→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    94→Just verify: is each issue real?
    95→
    96→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    97→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    98→
    99→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
   100→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
   101→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
   102→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   103→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   104→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   105→
   106→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
   107→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
   108→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
   109→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
   110→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
   111→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
   112→
   113→**What a GOOD observe report looks like:**
   114→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   115→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   116→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   117→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   118→
   119→**What a LAZY observe report looks like (will be rejected):**
   120→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   121→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   122→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   123→
   124→**Your report must include for EVERY issue:**
   125→1. The hash prefix
   126→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   127→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   128→
   129→When done, run:
   130→```
   131→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with [hash] citations>"
   132→```
   133→"""
   134→
   135→
   136→def _reflect_instructions() -> str:
   137→    return """\
   138→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   139→
   140→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   141→
   142→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   143→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   144→
   145→### What you must do:
   146→
   147→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from observe verdicts)?
   148→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   149→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   150→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? (with per-issue justification — "low priority" is
   151→   not a justification; "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication" is)
   152→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   153→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   154→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   155→   another round of fixes.
   156→
   157→### Your report MUST include a concrete cluster blueprint
   158→
   159→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   160→```
   161→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks": issues X, Y, Z (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   162→Cluster "task-typing": issues A, B (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   163→Skip: issue W (false positive per observe), issue V (over-engineering — fix adds 50 lines for 3 lines saved)
   164→```
   165→
   166→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   167→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   168→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   169→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   170→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   171→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   172→
   173→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   174→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   175→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   176→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   177→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   178→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering).
   179→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   180→
   181→When done, run:
   182→```
   183→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
   184→```
   185→"""
   186→
   187→
   188→def _organize_instructions() -> str:
   189→    return """\
   190→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   191→
   192→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   193→
   194→The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go
   195→where, what to skip. Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned
   196→(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why.
   197→
   198→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   199→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   200→
   201→### Process
   202→
   203→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint (provided below)
   204→2. **Skip false positives and over-engineering** identified in observe/reflect. Every skip needs a
   205→   per-issue justification — not "low priority" but "false positive: the code at line 47
   206→   already uses named constants, contradicting the issue's claim":
   207→   ```
   208→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<specific per-issue reason>" --attest "<attestation>"
   209→   ```
   210→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   211→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   212→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   213→5. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   214→6. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   215→7. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   216→
   217→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   218→
   219→Before recording, verify:
   220→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   221→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   222→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   223→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   224→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   225→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   226→
   227→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.
   228→
   229→When done, run:
   230→```
   231→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   232→```
   233→"""
   234→
   235→
   236→def _enrich_instructions() -> str:
   237→    return """\
   238→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   239→
   240→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   241→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   242→
   243→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   244→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   245→
   246→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   247→
   248→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   249→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   250→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   251→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   252→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   253→
   254→### How to enrich
   255→
   256→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   257→
   258→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get current steps and issue list
   259→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   260→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   261→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   262→   function name), and the exact change to make
   263→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   264→
   265→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   266→
   267→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   268→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   269→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   270→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   271→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   272→
   273→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   274→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   275→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   276→
   277→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   278→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   279→
   280→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   281→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   282→
   283→### Examples
   284→
   285→**GOOD step detail:**
   286→```
   287→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   288→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   289→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   290→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   291→```
   292→
   293→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   294→```
   295→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   296→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   297→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   298→```
   299→
   300→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   301→
   302→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   303→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   304→
   305→### File path rules
   306→
   307→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   308→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   309→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   310→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   311→
   312→When done, run:
   313→```
   314→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   315→```
   316→"""
   317→
   318→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {
   319→    "observe": _observe_instructions,
   320→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   321→    "organize": _organize_instructions,
   322→    "enrich": _enrich_instructions,
   323→}
   324→
   325→
   326→def build_stage_prompt(
   327→    stage: str,
   328→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   329→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   330→    *,
   331→    repo_root: Path,
   332→) -> str:
   333→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   334→    parts: list[str] = []
   335→
   336→    # Preamble
   337→    parts.append(_PREAMBLE.format(stage=stage.upper(), repo_root=repo_root))
   338→
   339→    # Issue data
   340→    issue_data = build_triage_prompt(triage_input)
   341→    parts.append("## Issue Data\n\n" + issue_data)
   342→
   343→    # Prior stage reports
   344→    if prior_reports:
   345→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   346→        for prior_stage, report in prior_reports.items():
   347→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   348→
   349→    # Stage-specific instructions
   350→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   351→    if instruction_fn:
   352→        parts.append(instruction_fn())
   353→
   354→    # CLI reference
   355→    parts.append(_CLI_REFERENCE)
   356→
   357→    # Validation requirements
   358→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   359→
   360→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   361→
   362→
   363→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   364→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   365→    *,
   366→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   367→) -> None:
   368→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   369→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   370→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   371→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   372→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   373→    state = runtime.state
   374→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   375→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   376→
   377→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   379→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   380→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   381→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   382→        if prior_stage == stage:
   383→            break
   384→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   385→        if report:
   386→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   387→
   388→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root)
   389→    print(prompt)
   390→
   391→
   392→def _validation_requirements(stage: str) -> str:
   393→    """What must be true for the stage to pass validation."""
   394→    if stage == "observe":
   395→        return (
   396→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   397→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   398→            "- Report must cite at least 10% of issue IDs (or 5, whichever is smaller)\n"
   399→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation mentioning dimension names\n"
   400→        )
   401→    if stage == "reflect":
   402→        return (
   403→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   404→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   405→            "- Report must mention recurring dimension names (if any exist)\n"
   406→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation\n"
   407→        )
   408→    if stage == "organize":
   409→        return (
   410→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   411→            "- At least one manual cluster with issues must exist\n"
   412→            "- All manual clusters must have description and action steps\n"
   413→            "- All review issues must be in a cluster or skipped\n"
   414→            "- Overlapping clusters must have --depends-on relationships\n"
   415→            "- Cluster descriptions must reflect current issues (not stale/skipped ones)\n"
   416→            "- Clusters must group by file/area proximity, not by dimension or theme\n"
   417→            "- A cluster whose issues span 5+ unrelated directories will be flagged\n"
   418→            "- Step count should be less than issue count (consolidate shared-file changes)\n"
   419→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   420→        )
   421→    if stage == "enrich":
   422→        return (
   423→            "## Validation Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — not advisory)\n"
   424→            "- Every step needs --detail with 80+ chars INCLUDING a file path\n"
   425→            "- Every step needs --issue-refs linking to review issue(s)\n"
   426→            "- Every step needs --effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)\n"
   427→            "- No bad file paths in step details (must exist on disk)\n"
   428→            "- No steps referencing skipped/wontfixed issues\n"
   429→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   430→        )
   431→    return ""
   432→
   433→
   434→def _observe_batch_instructions(issue_count: int, total_batches: int) -> str:
   435→    return f"""\
   436→## OBSERVE Batch Instructions
   437→
   438→You are one of {total_batches} parallel observe batches. Your task: verify every issue
   439→assigned to you against the actual source code.
   440→
   441→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
   442→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
   443→- Describe code that was already refactored
   444→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
   445→- Count props/returns/args wrong
   446→
   447→Your job is to catch these. A report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
   448→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
   449→
   450→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. Just verify: is each issue real?
   451→
   452→**For EVERY issue you must:**
   453→- Open and read the actual source file
   454→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   455→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   456→- Report a clear verdict: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   457→
   458→**What a GOOD report looks like:**
   459→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   460→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   461→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   462→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   463→
   464→**What a LAZY report looks like (will be rejected):**
   465→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   466→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   467→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   468→
   469→**Your report must include for EVERY issue ({issue_count} total):**
   470→1. The hash prefix in brackets
   471→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   472→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   473→
   474→## IMPORTANT: Output Rules
   475→
   476→**Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands.** Do NOT run `desloppify plan triage --stage observe`.
   477→You are a parallel batch — the orchestrator will merge all batch outputs and record the stage.
   478→
   479→**Write your analysis as plain text only.** Format:
   480→```
   481→[hash_prefix] VERDICT — evidence
   482→```
   483→"""
   484→
   485→
   486→def build_observe_batch_prompt(
   487→    batch_index: int,
   488→    total_batches: int,
   489→    dimension_group: list[str],
   490→    issues_subset: dict[str, dict],
   491→    *,
   492→    repo_root: Path,
   493→) -> str:
   494→    """Build a scoped observe prompt for a single dimension-group batch.
   495→
   496→    Unlike build_stage_prompt(), this produces a prompt for observe only,
   497→    scoped to a subset of issues. The batch subprocess writes analysis to
   498→    stdout — it does NOT run ``desloppify plan triage --stage observe``.
   499→    The orchestrator merges batch outputs and records observe once.
   500→    """
   501→    parts: list[str] = []
   502→
   503→    # Batch context header
   504→    parts.append(
   505→        f"You are observe batch {batch_index}/{total_batches}.\n"
   506→        f"Dimensions assigned to you: {', '.join(dimension_group)}\n"
   507→        f"Total issues in this batch: {len(issues_subset)}\n\n"
   508→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   509→    )
   510→
   511→    # Issue data — inline the subset directly
   512→    parts.append("## Issues to Verify\n")
   513→    for fid, f in sorted(issues_subset.items()):
   514→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   515→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   516→        title = f.get("title", fid)
   517→        file_path = detail.get("file_path", "")
   518→        description = detail.get("description", f.get("description", ""))
   519→        line = f"- [{fid[:8]}] ({dim}) **{title}**"
   520→        if file_path:
   521→            line += f" — `{file_path}`"
   522→        if description:
   523→            line += f"\n  {description[:300]}"
   524→        parts.append(line)
   525→
   526→    # Batch-specific observe instructions (no subagent/CLI references)
   527→    parts.append(_observe_batch_instructions(len(issues_subset), total_batches))
   528→
   529→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   530→
   531→
   532→__all__ = ["build_observe_batch_prompt", "build_stage_prompt", "cmd_stage_prompt"]
   533→
```
