   214→    if len(report) < 100:
   215→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   216→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   217→        return None
   218→    return report
   219→
   220→
   221→def _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages: dict) -> bool:
   222→    """Gate: organize must be done before enrich."""
   223→    if "organize" in stages:
   224→        return True
   225→    if "observe" not in stages:
   226→        print(colorize("  Cannot enrich: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   227→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   228→        return False
   229→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   230→        print(colorize("  Cannot enrich: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   231→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   232→        return False
   233→    print(colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not complete.", "red"))
   234→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
   235→    return False
   236→
   237→
   238→def _shallow_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   239→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for clusters with steps lacking detail."""
   240→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   241→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   242→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   243→            continue
   244→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   245→        if not steps:
   246→            continue
   247→        bare = sum(
   248→            1 for s in steps
   249→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("detail") and not s.get("issue_refs")
   250→        )
   251→        if bare > 0:
   252→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
   253→    return results
   254→
   255→
   256→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   257→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
   258→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   259→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   260→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   261→            continue
   262→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   264→                continue
   265→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   266→            if not detail:
   267→                continue
   268→            bad: list[str] = []
   269→            for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(detail):
   270→                p = repo_root / path_str
   271→                if p.exists():
   272→                    continue
   273→                alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(p.suffix)
   274→                if alt_ext and p.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists():
   275→                    continue
   276→                bad.append(path_str)
   277→            if bad:
   278→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   279→    return results
   280→
   281→
   282→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   283→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   284→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   285→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   286→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   287→            continue
   288→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   289→        if not steps:
   290→            continue
   291→        missing = sum(
   292→            1 for s in steps
   293→            if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   294→        )
   295→        if missing:
   296→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   297→    return results
   298→
   299→
   300→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   301→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   302→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   303→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   304→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   305→            continue
   306→        paths: set[str] = set()
   307→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   308→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   309→                paths.update(_PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]))
   310→        if paths:
   311→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   312→
   313→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   314→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   315→    for i, a in enumerate(names):
   316→        for b in names[i + 1:]:
   317→            shared = cluster_files[a] & cluster_files[b]
   318→            if shared:
   319→                overlaps.append((a, b, sorted(shared)))
   320→    return overlaps
   321→
   322→
   323→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   324→    plan: dict, *, threshold: int = 5,
   325→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   326→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories.
   327→
   328→    A cluster with issues in 5+ distinct top-level directories is likely
   329→    grouped by theme/dimension rather than by file proximity.
   330→    Returns (cluster_name, dir_count, sample_dirs).
   331→    """
   332→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   333→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   334→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   335→            continue
   336→        # Collect file paths from step details
   337→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   338→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   339→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   340→                for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]):
   341→                    # Use first 2 path components as the "area"
   342→                    # e.g., src/domains/media-lightbox -> src/domains/media-lightbox
   343→                    parts = path_str.split("/")
   344→                    if len(parts) >= 3:
   345→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   346→                    elif len(parts) >= 2:
   347→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   348→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   349→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   350→    return results
   351→
   352→
   353→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   354→    plan: dict, *, max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   355→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   356→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping).
   357→
   358→    A well-organized cluster should consolidate related issues into fewer
   359→    steps (because multiple issues touching the same file become one step).
   360→    Returns (cluster_name, steps, issues, ratio).
   361→    """
   362→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   363→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   364→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   365→            continue
   366→        steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
   367→        issues = len(cluster.get("issue_ids") or [])
   368→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:  # only flag non-trivial clusters
   369→            ratio = steps / issues
   370→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   371→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   372→    return results
   373→
   374→
   375→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   376→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   377→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   378→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   379→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   380→            continue
   381→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   382→        if not steps:
   383→            continue
   384→        missing = sum(
   385→            1 for s in steps
   386→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("issue_refs")
   387→        )
   388→        if missing:
   389→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   390→    return results
   391→
   392→
   393→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   394→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready.
   395→
   396→    A step is "vague" if its detail is under 80 chars AND contains no file path.
   397→    Executor-ready means: someone with zero context can read the step and know
   398→    exactly which file to open and what to change.
   399→    """
   400→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   401→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   402→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   403→            continue
   404→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   405→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   406→                continue
   407→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   408→            if not detail:
   409→                continue
   410→            has_path = bool(_PATH_RE.search(detail))
   411→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   412→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   413→    return results
   414→
   415→
   416→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   417→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   418→    wontfixed = set()
   419→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   420→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   421→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   422→    # Also check the wontfix list directly
   423→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   424→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   425→
   426→    if not wontfixed:
   427→        return []
   428→
   429→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   430→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   431→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   432→            continue
   433→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   434→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   435→                continue
   436→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   437→            stale = [r for r in refs if r in wontfixed]
   438→            if stale:
   439→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   440→    return results
   441→
   442→
   443→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   444→    if not report:
   445→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   446→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   448→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   449→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   450→        return None
   451→    if len(report) < 100:
   452→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   453→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   454→        return None
   455→    return report
   456→
   457→
   458→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   459→    *,
   460→    plan: dict,
   461→    meta: dict,
   462→    stages: dict,
   463→) -> bool:
   464→    if "enrich" in stages:
   465→        return True
   466→    if "organize" not in stages:
   467→        # Fall through to existing organize requirement
   468→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   469→
   470→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   471→    if shallow:
   472→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
   473→        print(colorize(f"  {len(shallow)} cluster(s) have steps without detail or issue_refs:", "yellow"))
   474→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   475→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   476→        print(colorize(
   477→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   478→            "dim",
   479→        ))
   480→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   481→    else:
   482→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   483→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
   484→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   485→    return False
   486→
   487→
   488→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   489→    *,
   490→    plan: dict,
   491→    stages: dict,
   492→    attestation: str | None,
   493→) -> bool:
   494→    if stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   495→        return True
   496→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   497→        return False
   498→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   499→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   500→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   501→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.", "dim"))
   502→        return False
   503→
   504→    # Re-validate shallow steps at auto-confirm time
   505→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   506→    if shallow:
   507→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   508→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   509→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   510→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   511→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   512→        return False
   513→
   514→    cluster_names = [
   515→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   516→    ]
   517→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   518→        attestation.strip(),
   519→        "enrich",
   520→        cluster_names=cluster_names,
   521→    )
   522→    if validation_err:
   523→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   524→        return False
   525→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   526→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   527→    save_plan(plan)
   528→    print(colorize("  ✓ Enrich auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   529→    return True
   530→
   531→
   532→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   533→    *,
   534→    plan: dict,
   535→    meta: dict,
   536→    stages: dict,
   537→) -> bool:
   538→    if "organize" in stages:
   539→        return True
   540→    if "observe" not in stages:
   541→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   542→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   543→        return False
   544→
   545→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   546→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   547→    if gaps:
   548→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   549→        for name, missing in gaps:
   550→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   551→        print(
   552→            colorize(
   553→                '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   554→                "dim",
   555→            )
   556→        )
   557→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   558→    else:
   559→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   560→        if manual:
   561→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   562→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   563→        else:
   564→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   565→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   566→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   567→        print(
   568→            colorize(
   569→                '  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."',
   570→                "dim",
   571→            )
   572→        )
   573→    return False
   574→
   575→
   576→def _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
   577→    *,
   578→    plan: dict,
   579→    stages: dict,
   580→    attestation: str | None,
   581→) -> bool:
   582→    if stages["organize"].get("confirmed_at"):
   583→        return True
   584→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   585→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   586→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   587→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
   588→        return False
   589→
   590→    organize_clusters = [
   591→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   592→    ]
   593→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   594→        attestation.strip(),
   595→        "organize",
   596→        cluster_names=organize_clusters,
   597→    )
   598→    if validation_err:
   599→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   600→        return False
   601→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   602→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   603→    save_plan(plan)
   604→    print(colorize("  ✓ Organize auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   605→    return True
   606→
   607→
   608→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
   609→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   610→    if not manual_clusters:
   611→        any_clusters = [
   612→            name
   613→            for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   614→            if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   615→        ]
   616→        if not any_clusters:
   617→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   618→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   619→            return False
   620→
   621→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   622→    if gaps:
   623→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
   624→        for name, missing in gaps:
   625→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   626→        print(
   627→            colorize(
   628→                "  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.",
   629→                "dim",
   630→            )
   631→        )
   632→        print(
   633→            colorize(
   634→                '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   635→                "dim",
   636→            )
   637→        )
   638→        return False
   639→
   640→    # Check for unclustered review issues
   641→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   642→    if unclustered:
   643→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
   644→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:
   645→            short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   646→            print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   647→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
   648→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
   649→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
   650→        return False
   651→
   652→    return True
   653→
   654→
   655→def _resolve_completion_strategy(
   656→    strategy: str | None,
   657→    *,
   658→    meta: dict,
   659→) -> str | None:
   660→    if strategy:
   661→        return strategy
   662→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   663→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   664→    if existing:
   665→        print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
   666→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   667→    else:
   668→        print(
   669→            colorize(
   670→                '  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"',
   671→                "dim",
   672→            )
   673→        )
   674→    return None
   675→
   676→
   677→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   678→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   679→        return True
   680→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   681→        return True
   682→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   683→    print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
   684→    print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
   685→    print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
   686→    print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
   687→    return False
   688→

   690→def _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta: dict) -> bool:
   691→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
   692→        return True
   693→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
   694→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   695→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   696→    return False

   698→
   699→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   700→    *,
   701→    stages: dict,
   702→    has_only_additions: bool,
   703→    si,
   704→) -> bool:
   705→    if has_only_additions:
   706→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)
   707→        return True
   708→    if "observe" not in stages:
   709→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   710→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   711→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   712→        return False
   713→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   714→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   715→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   716→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   717→        return False
   718→    return True
   719→

   721→def _confirm_note_valid(note: str | None) -> bool:
   722→    if not note:
   723→        print(colorize("  --note is required for confirm-existing.", "red"))
   724→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   725→        return False
   726→    if len(note) < 100:
   727→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   728→        return False
   729→    return True
   730→
   731→
   732→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   733→    strategy: str | None,
   734→    *,
   735→    has_only_additions: bool,
   736→    meta: dict,
   737→) -> str | None:
   738→    if strategy:
   739→        return strategy
   740→    if has_only_additions:
   741→        return "same"
   742→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   743→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   744→    if existing:
   745→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   746→    return None
   747→
   748→
   749→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   750→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   751→        return True
   752→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   753→        return True
   754→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   755→    return False
   756→
   757→
   758→def _confirmed_text_or_error(
   759→    *,
   760→    plan: dict,
   761→    state: dict,
   762→    confirmed: str | None,
   763→) -> str | None:
   764→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   765→        return confirmed.strip()
   766→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   767→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   768→    if confirmed:
   769→        print(
   770→            colorize(
   771→                f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   772→                "red",
   773→            )
   774→        )
   775→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   776→    return None
   777→
   778→
   779→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   780→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   781→    if not new_ids:
   782→        return True
   783→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   784→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   785→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   786→    if new_cited:
   787→        return True
   788→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   789→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   790→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   791→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   792→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   793→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   794→    return False
   795→
   796→
   797→__all__ = [

1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    12→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    13→    purge_ids,
    14→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    17→
    18→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    19→
    20→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]
    21→
    22→
    23→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    24→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    25→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    26→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    27→
    28→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    29→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    30→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    31→    existing = set(order)
    32→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    33→        if sid not in existing:
    34→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    35→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    36→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    37→        if sid in order:
    38→            order.remove(sid)
    39→    insert_at = 0
    40→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    41→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    42→        insert_at += 1
    43→
    44→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    45→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    46→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    47→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    48→
    49→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    51→    try:
    52→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    53→    except ValueError:
    54→        return []
    55→    cleared: list[str] = []
    56→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    57→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    60→            cleared.append(later)
    61→    return cleared
    62→
    63→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    64→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    65→    if not cleared:
    66→        return
    67→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    68→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    69→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    70→        None,
    71→    )
    72→    if next_unconfirmed:
    73→        print(colorize(
    74→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    75→            "dim",
    76→        ))
    77→
    78→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    79→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    80→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    81→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    82→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    83→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    84→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    85→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    86→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    87→
    88→def group_issues_into_observe_batches(
    89→    si,
    90→    max_batches: int = 5,
    91→) -> list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]]:
    92→    """Group issues by dimension into batches for parallel observe.
    93→
    94→    Returns list of (dimension_names, issues_subset) tuples.
    95→    Single batch if only one dimension exists.
    96→    """
    97→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    98→
    99→    if len(dim_names) <= 1:
   100→        return [(dim_names, dict(si.open_issues))]
   101→
   102→    # Distribute dimensions into balanced batches by issue count
   103→    num_batches = min(max_batches, len(dim_names))
   104→    batch_dims: list[list[str]] = [[] for _ in range(num_batches)]
   105→    batch_counts: list[int] = [0] * num_batches
   106→
   107→    # Greedy: assign each dimension (largest first) to the lightest batch
   108→    for dim in dim_names:
   109→        lightest = min(range(num_batches), key=lambda i: batch_counts[i])
   110→        batch_dims[lightest].append(dim)
   111→        batch_counts[lightest] += by_dim[dim]
   112→
   113→    # Build issue subsets per batch
   114→    # Pre-index issues by dimension
   115→    dim_to_issues: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = defaultdict(dict)
   116→    for fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
   117→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   119→        dim_to_issues[dim][fid] = f
   120→
   121→    result: list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]] = []
   122→    for dims in batch_dims:
   123→        if not dims:
   124→            continue
   125→        subset: dict[str, dict] = {}
   126→        for dim in dims:
   127→            subset.update(dim_to_issues.get(dim, {}))
   128→        if subset:
   129→            result.append((dims, subset))
   130→
   131→    return result
   132→
   133→
   134→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
   135→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
   136→    return {
   137→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   138→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   139→    }
   140→
   141→def triage_coverage(
   142→    plan: dict,
   143→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   144→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
   145→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
   146→
   147→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   148→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   149→    """
   150→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   151→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   152→    for c in clusters.values():
   153→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   154→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   155→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   156→    else:
   157→        review_ids = [
   158→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   159→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   160→        ]
   161→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   162→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   163→
   164→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   165→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   166→    return [
   167→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   168→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   169→    ]
   170→
   171→def apply_completion(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    plan: dict,
   174→    strategy: str,
   175→    *,
   176→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   177→) -> None:
   178→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   179→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   180→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   181→    state = runtime.state
   182→
   183→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(
   184→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   185→    )
   186→
   187→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   188→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   189→
   190→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   193→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   194→    open_ids = sorted(
   195→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   196→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   197→    )
   198→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_ids
   199→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   200→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   201→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   202→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   203→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   204→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   205→    if stages:
   206→        meta["last_triage"] = {
   207→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   208→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   209→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   210→        }
   211→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   212→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   213→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   214→
   215→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   216→
   217→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("issue_ids")])
   218→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   219→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   220→    if effective_strategy:
   221→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   222→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   223→
   224→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
   225→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
   226→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   227→        if fid in c.get("issue_ids", []):
   228→            return name
   229→    return None
   230→
   231→def count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   232→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
   233→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   234→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   235→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
   236→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
   237→    return dict(counts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "apply_completion",
   241→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   242→    "count_log_activity_since",
   243→    "find_cluster_for",
   244→    "group_issues_into_observe_batches",
   245→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   246→    "inject_triage_stages",
   247→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   248→    "observe_dimension_breakdown",
   249→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   250→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",

     2→

     5→import time

     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

     9→
    10→BatchTask = Callable[[], int]
    11→
    12→
    13→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    14→class BatchProgressEvent:
    15→    """Typed progress event emitted by batch runner execution."""
    16→
    17→    batch_index: int
    18→    event: str
    19→    code: int | None = None

    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class BatchExecutionOptions:
    25→    """Runtime options for serial/parallel batch execution."""
    26→
    27→    run_parallel: bool
    28→    max_parallel_workers: int | None = None
    29→    heartbeat_seconds: float | None = 15.0
    30→    clock_fn: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    34→class BatchResult:
    35→    """Typed normalized batch payload passed to merge/import stages."""
    36→
    37→    batch_index: int

    39→    dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    40→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]]
    41→    quality: dict[str, float]
    42→
    43→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    44→        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    45→            "assessments": self.assessments,
    46→            "dimension_notes": self.dimension_notes,
    47→            "issues": self.issues,
    48→            "quality": self.quality,
    49→        }
    50→        payload["batch_index"] = self.batch_index
    51→        return payload
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = [
    55→    "BatchExecutionOptions",
    56→    "BatchProgressEvent",
    57→    "BatchResult",

1→"""Cluster mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import move_items
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→
    10→def create_cluster(
    11→    plan: PlanModel,
    12→    name: str,
    13→    description: str | None = None,
    14→    action: str | None = None,
    15→) -> Cluster:
    16→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
    19→        raise ValueError(
    20→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
    21→        )
    22→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
    23→        raise ValueError(
    24→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
    25→        )
    26→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
    27→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
    28→    now = utc_now()
    29→    cluster: Cluster = {
    30→        "name": name,
    31→        "description": description,
    32→        "issue_ids": [],
    33→        "created_at": now,
    34→        "updated_at": now,
    35→        "auto": False,
    36→        "cluster_key": "",
    37→        "action": action,
    38→        "user_modified": False,
    39→    }
    40→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
    41→    return cluster
    42→
    43→
    44→def add_to_cluster(
    45→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    46→) -> int:
    47→    """Add issue IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
    48→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    49→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
    50→    if cluster is None:
    51→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    52→
    53→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    54→    count = 0
    55→    now = utc_now()
    56→    for fid in issue_ids:
    57→        if fid not in member_ids:
    58→            member_ids.append(fid)
    59→            count += 1
    60→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
    61→        if fid not in overrides:
    62→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    63→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
    64→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
    65→
    66→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
    67→    return count
    68→
    69→
    70→def remove_from_cluster(
    71→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    72→) -> int:
    73→    """Remove issue IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
    74→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

    76→    if cluster is None:
    77→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    78→
    79→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    80→    now = utc_now()

Now I have a complete picture. Let me design the sense-check architecture.

---

## Sense-Check Stage Architecture Design

### Overview

The sense-check stage sits between `enrich` and `commit` in the triage pipeline. Its purpose is to catch every failure mode that would cause an executor ("code monkey") to waste time, get confused, or break things. Unlike the existing validation in `stage_validation.py` which performs structural/syntactic checks (are fields populated? do paths exist?), sense-check performs **semantic** verification: are the instructions actually correct, complete, and safe?

The existing enrich validation already catches:
- Missing detail/issue_refs/effort (structural)
- Non-existent file paths (filesystem check)
- Vague detail under 80 chars (length heuristic)

Sense-check catches what remains: **is the content of those fields actually right?**

---

### 1. Three Subagents

#### Subagent A: "Ground Truth Verifier"

**Responsibility:** Verify every factual claim in step details against the actual codebase.

**What it reads:**
- The enriched plan (all clusters with action_steps)
- Every source file referenced in step details
- The state/issues data to cross-reference issue descriptions

**What it checks (failure modes):**
- File paths exist but the described code is not at the claimed location (e.g., "lines 45-89 contain filter state" but filter state is at lines 120-160 now)
- Function/variable names mentioned in step detail do not exist in the referenced file
- Counts are wrong ("3 imports to update" but there are actually 5)
- Code described in a step was already changed (issue was fixed between scan and triage)
- The issue referenced by `issue_refs` describes a problem that is no longer present in the file
- Step says "delete lines 12-18" but those lines contain something completely different than what the step implies

**What it can fix:**
- Correct line number ranges to match current file state
- Update function/variable name references
- Correct counts ("update the 3 imports" -> "update the 5 imports")
- Flag steps as stale/resolved if the underlying issue no longer exists (add a `sense_check_note` field or remove the step)
- Update file paths if a file was moved (check git log)

**Output format:** A JSON report per cluster with a list of findings:
```python
{
    "cluster_name": str,
    "step_index": int,
    "finding_type": "line_drift" | "name_mismatch" | "count_wrong" | "already_resolved" | "code_changed",
    "severity": "error" | "warning",
    "current_truth": str,   # what we actually found
    "step_claimed": str,    # what the step says
    "fix_applied": bool,
    "fix_description": str | None,
    "cli_command": str | None,  # the desloppify CLI command to apply the fix
}
```

#### Subagent B: "Dependency & Sequencing Auditor"

**Responsibility:** Verify that steps within each cluster and clusters relative to each other are in a safe execution order.

**What it reads:**
- The enriched plan (clusters, action_steps, `depends_on_clusters`)
- The actual files referenced by steps (to understand import/export relationships)

**What it checks (failure modes):**
- Step N modifies a function signature that Step M (later in the same cluster) calls -- but Step M's detail assumes the old signature
- Two steps in the same cluster modify the same file section (merge conflict risk) and are not adjacent
- Cluster A modifies an export that Cluster B imports, but `depends_on_clusters` does not reflect this
- Cluster A and Cluster B both modify the same file but have no dependency relationship
- A step adds a new import from a file that a later step in a different cluster deletes or renames
- Circular dependencies between clusters (already partially checked in `validate_completion`)
- A "trivial" step depends on understanding the output of a "large" step before it (implicit sequencing)

**What it can fix:**
- Reorder steps within a cluster to resolve intra-cluster sequencing issues
- Add `depends_on_clusters` relationships between clusters that share file references
- Split a step that touches multiple concerns into sequential sub-steps
- Flag ordering issues that require human decision (cannot auto-fix: "Cluster A should probably run before Cluster B, but this reverses the current priority order")

**Output format:** A JSON report:
```python
{
    "intra_cluster_issues": [
        {
            "cluster_name": str,
            "issue_type": "step_order" | "same_file_conflict" | "implicit_dependency",
            "step_indices": list[int],
            "description": str,
            "fix_applied": bool,
            "fix_description": str | None,
        }
    ],
    "inter_cluster_issues": [
        {
            "cluster_a": str,
            "cluster_b": str,
            "issue_type": "shared_file" | "import_export" | "missing_dependency" | "circular",
            "shared_files": list[str],
            "fix_applied": bool,
            "fix_description": str | None,
            "cli_command": str | None,
        }
    ],
}
```

#### Subagent C: "Executor Readiness Auditor"

**Responsibility:** Determine whether each step can be executed by someone with zero codebase context, literally and mechanically.

**What it reads:**
- The enriched plan (all clusters with action_steps)
- Source files referenced in step details (to validate that instructions are sufficient)

**What it checks (failure modes):**
- Step says "extract into a new hook" but does not specify: the new file name, the exact function signature, what the return type should be, which imports to move
- Step says "update the 3 imports" but does not list which files contain those imports
- Step uses subjective language ("clean up", "improve", "refactor appropriately") without specifying the exact transformation
- Step references a concept without explaining it ("use the branded string pattern" -- what pattern? where is it defined?)
- Effort tag is wrong: step says "trivial" but requires modifying 5+ files, or says "large" but is a one-line rename
- Duplicate steps across clusters (two steps in different clusters that would make the same change to the same file)
- Missing steps: an issue is in `issue_refs` but the step's instructions do not actually address what the issue describes
- Steps that would break other code: step renames a function but does not mention updating all call sites
- Over-specified steps that are brittle: "delete line 47" when the surrounding context suggests the executor should understand WHY line 47 is being deleted

**What it can fix:**
- Expand vague steps with specific file paths, function names, and exact transformations (by reading the code)
- Correct effort tags based on actual file count and change scope
- Deduplicate steps across clusters (flag the duplicate, suggest removal via CLI command)
- Add missing call-site update instructions when a step renames/removes an export
- Add the "which files to update" list when a step says "update imports" without listing them

**Output format:** A JSON report per step:
```python
{
    "cluster_name": str,
    "step_index": int,
    "readiness_score": float,  # 0.0 to 1.0
    "issues": [
        {
            "issue_type": "vague_action" | "missing_target_files" | "wrong_effort" | "duplicate_work" | "missing_callsite_updates" | "unaddressed_issue",
            "severity": "error" | "warning",
            "description": str,
            "fix_applied": bool,
            "fix_description": str | None,
            "cli_command": str | None,
        }
    ],
}
```

---

### 2. Parallelism Strategy

**Subagents A and C can run in parallel.** They both read the plan and source files but do not modify the plan. They produce independent reports.

**Subagent B must also run in parallel with A and C** for the read-only analysis phase, but its fixes (reordering steps, adding `depends_on`) must be applied after A and C's fixes, because A might remove stale steps and C might add new steps, both of which change the step indices that B references.

However, the more practical architecture is:

**Phase 1 (parallel):** All three subagents run simultaneously. Each produces a findings report (JSON). None apply fixes yet.

**Phase 2 (sequential, deterministic):** A merge/fix stage runs in the orchestrator (not a subagent -- pure Python logic) that:
1. Applies Subagent A fixes first (ground truth corrections -- these change step content but not structure)
2. Applies Subagent C fixes next (readiness improvements -- these may add detail or flag duplicates)
3. Applies Subagent B fixes last (reordering/dependency changes -- these change structure)

This two-phase design means subagents never need to coordinate, and the merge logic is deterministic and testable.

**Parallelization within subagents:** Each subagent should process clusters independently. Subagent A can launch sub-subagents per cluster (same pattern as observe batches). Subagent C similarly. Subagent B needs a global view for inter-cluster analysis, but intra-cluster analysis can be parallelized.

---

### 3. Merged Output and Plan Mutations

The sense-check stage produces a **SenseCheckReport** that gets persisted alongside the triage stages:

```python
class SenseCheckReport(TypedDict, total=False):
    """Persisted sense-check results from between enrich and commit."""
    timestamp: str
    ground_truth: dict       # Subagent A report
    sequencing: dict         # Subagent B report
    executor_readiness: dict # Subagent C report
    fixes_applied: list[dict]  # ordered list of mutations made
    errors_remaining: int    # count of unfixed errors
    warnings_remaining: int  # count of unfixed warnings
    pass_verdict: bool       # True if plan is safe to commit
```

This gets stored in `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["sense_check"]`.

The orchestrator in `_run_codex_pipeline` adds sense-check between the enrich confirmation and the auto-complete logic. The flow becomes:

```
for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"):
    ... existing pipeline ...

# NEW: sense-check after enrich is confirmed
ok, report = _run_sense_check(plan, state, repo_root, ...)
if not ok:
    # retry or abort
    ...

# existing: auto-complete triage
```

---

### 4. Validation Gates That Block Confirmation

The sense-check itself has a pass/fail gate. The following block the pipeline from proceeding to `commit`:

**Hard blockers (errors):**
1. Any step references a file path that exists but contains none of the described code (ground truth failure after attempting fix)
2. Any step's `issue_refs` point to issues that are already resolved/no longer present in state
3. Circular cluster dependencies detected
4. Two clusters modify the same file with no `depends_on_clusters` relationship AND the subagent could not determine safe ordering
5. A step has a `readiness_score` below 0.5 after fix attempts (still too vague to execute)
6. Duplicate steps exist across clusters that were not resolvable (both seem necessary in their respective cluster contexts)

**Soft warnings (logged but non-blocking):**
1. Line number drift corrected (the fix was applied, but it indicates enrich quality was low)
2. Effort tags adjusted (again, enrich quality signal)
3. Additional call-site update instructions added to a step
4. Steps reordered within a cluster

The `validate_stage("sense_check", ...)` function checks:
- `errors_remaining == 0`
- `pass_verdict is True`

---

### 5. Retry Mechanism

If sense-check finds too many problems, the pipeline should retry enrich rather than trying to patch a fundamentally broken plan. The threshold:

**Retry enrich if:**
- More than 30% of steps have ground truth errors (the enrich subagent was hallucinating)
- More than 50% of steps have readiness scores below 0.7 (the enrich subagent was lazy)
- Any cluster has ALL steps flagged as problematic

**Retry sense-check subagents if:**
- A subagent crashes or times out (same retry logic as existing `codex_runner.py` with `max_retries=1`)
- A subagent produces no output or malformed JSON

**Maximum retry depth:**
- 1 retry of individual sense-check subagents (per subagent)
- 1 retry of the entire enrich + sense-check loop
- After that, abort the pipeline with a detailed error report pointing the user to the run artifacts

The retry is implemented as a loop in the orchestrator:

```python
for attempt in range(MAX_SENSE_CHECK_ATTEMPTS):  # 2
    ok, report = _run_sense_check(...)
    if ok:
        break
    if report["errors_remaining"] > threshold:
        # Re-run enrich with the sense-check findings as additional context
        _rerun_enrich_with_feedback(report, ...)
    else:
        # Sense-check can try to fix remaining issues itself
        _run_sense_check_fixup(report, ...)
```

---

### 6. Implementation Plan

**Step 1: Define the sense-check report schema**
Add `SenseCheckFinding`, `SenseCheckClusterReport`, and `SenseCheckReport` TypedDicts to `schema.py`. Add `sense_check` to `EpicTriageMeta`.

**Step 2: Add "sense_check" to the stage infrastructure**
Update `TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS` and `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` in `triage_playbook.py` to include sense_check between enrich and commit. Update `_STAGES` tuple in `orchestrator.py`.

**Step 3: Create the subagent prompt builders**
Add `_sense_check_ground_truth_instructions()`, `_sense_check_sequencing_instructions()`, and `_sense_check_readiness_instructions()` to `stage_prompts.py`. Each produces a focused prompt that includes the enriched plan data and instructs the subagent to output structured JSON findings.

**Step 4: Create the sense-check orchestration function**
In `orchestrator.py`, add `_run_sense_check()` that follows the same pattern as `_run_observe()`: builds prompts, launches parallel codex subprocesses via `execute_batches`, collects outputs, parses JSON reports.

**Step 5: Create the merge/fix logic**
New file: `runner/sense_check_merge.py`. Pure Python functions that take the three subagent reports and the current plan, apply fixes in order (A then C then B), and produce the final `SenseCheckReport`. This is the key testable unit -- no subprocess calls, just plan mutations.

**Step 6: Create the sense-check validation function**
In `runner/stage_validation.py`, add `validate_sense_check()` that checks `errors_remaining == 0` and `pass_verdict is True`.

**Step 7: Wire into the pipeline**
In `_run_codex_pipeline()`, after the enrich stage is confirmed and before auto-complete, call `_run_sense_check()`. Add retry logic.

**Step 8: Tests**
- Unit tests for `sense_check_merge.py` (given mock reports, verify correct plan mutations)
- Unit tests for each validation check (given a plan with known issues, verify detection)
- Integration test for the full sense-check flow with mock codex runner

---

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/orchestrator.py` - Main pipeline loop where sense-check gets wired in between enrich confirmation and auto-complete
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts.py` - Where the three subagent prompt builders will be added, following the existing pattern of `_observe_instructions()` etc.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/_stage_validation.py` - Contains all the existing validation functions (`_steps_with_bad_paths`, `_shallow_steps`, etc.) that sense-check builds upon and extends
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_validation.py` - The runner-level `validate_stage()` function that needs a new `sense_check` branch, plus the pass/fail gate logic
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` - Schema types (`ActionStep`, `Cluster`, `EpicTriageMeta`) that need the `SenseCheckReport` addition and the `sense_check` field on `EpicTriageMeta`

1→"""State mutation helpers for triage stage records."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import utc_now
     6→
     7→from .helpers import cascade_clear_later_confirmations
     8→
     9→
    10→def _resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def _record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→) -> list[str]:
    30→    stages["observe"] = {
    31→        "stage": "observe",
    32→        "report": report,
    33→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    34→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    35→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    36→    }
    37→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    38→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    39→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    40→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    41→    if not is_reuse:
    42→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    43→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    44→    return cleared
    45→
    46→
    47→def _record_organize_stage(
    48→    stages: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    report: str,
    51→    issue_count: int,
    52→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    53→    is_reuse: bool,
    54→) -> list[str]:
    55→    stages["organize"] = {
    56→        "stage": "organize",
    57→        "report": report,
    58→        "cited_ids": [],
    59→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    60→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    61→    }
    62→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    63→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    64→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    65→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _record_enrich_stage(
    69→    stages: dict,
    70→    *,
    71→    report: str,
    72→    shallow_count: int,
    73→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    74→    is_reuse: bool,
    75→) -> list[str]:
    76→    stages["enrich"] = {
    77→        "stage": "enrich",
    78→        "report": report,
    79→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    80→        "shallow_count": shallow_count,
    81→    }
    82→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    83→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    84→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    85→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "enrich")
    86→
    87→
    88→def _record_confirm_existing_completion(
    89→    *,
    90→    stages: dict,
    91→    note: str,
    92→    issue_count: int,
    93→    confirmed_text: str,
    94→) -> None:
    95→    stages["organize"] = {
    96→        "stage": "organize",
    97→        "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
    98→        "cited_ids": [],
    99→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   100→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   101→        "confirmed_at": utc_now(),
   102→        "confirmed_text": confirmed_text,
   103→    }
   104→
   105→
   106→__all__ = [
   107→    "_record_confirm_existing_completion",
   108→    "_record_enrich_stage",
   109→    "_record_observe_stage",
   110→    "_record_organize_stage",
   111→    "_resolve_reusable_report",
   112→]

     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    23→from .stage_helpers import _unclustered_review_issues, _unenriched_clusters

    27→_EXT_SWAPS = {'.ts': '.tsx', '.tsx': '.ts', '.js': '.jsx', '.jsx': '.js'}
    28→_VALID_EFFORTS = {"trivial", "small", "medium", "large"}

    30→
    31→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    32→    *,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    stages: dict,
    35→    attestation: str | None,
    36→    triage_input,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    39→        return True
    40→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
    41→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    42→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
    43→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.", "dim"))
    44→        return False
    45→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
    46→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
    47→        attestation.strip(),
    48→        "observe",
    49→        dimensions=dim_names,
    50→    )
    51→    if validation_err:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
    53→        return False
    54→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    55→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
    56→    save_plan(plan)
    57→    print(colorize("  ✓ Observe auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
    58→    return True
    59→
    60→
    61→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    62→    *,
    63→    report: str,
    64→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    65→    recurring: dict,
    66→) -> bool:
    67→    if not recurring_dims:
    68→        return True
    69→    report_lower = report.lower()
    70→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
    71→    if mentioned:
    72→        return True
    73→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
    74→    for dim in recurring_dims:
    75→        info = recurring[dim]
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
    79→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
    80→                "yellow",
    81→            )
    82→        )
    83→    print(
    84→        colorize(
    85→            "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→    return False
    90→
    91→
    92→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
    93→    if "reflect" in stages:
    94→        return True
    95→    if "observe" not in stages:
    96→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    97→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    98→        return False
    99→    print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   100→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   101→    return False

   104→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   105→    *,
   106→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   107→    plan: dict,
   108→    stages: dict,
   109→    attestation: str | None,
   110→) -> bool:
   111→    if stages["reflect"].get("confirmed_at"):
   112→        return True
   113→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   114→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   115→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   116→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.", "dim"))
   117→        return False
   118→
   119→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   120→    triage_input = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   121→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(
   122→        triage_input.open_issues,
   123→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   124→    )
   125→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   126→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   127→    reflect_clusters = [
   128→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   129→    ]
   130→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   131→        attestation.strip(),
   132→        "reflect",
   133→        dimensions=reflect_dims,
   134→        cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   135→    )
   136→    if validation_err:
   137→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   138→        return False
   139→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   140→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   141→    save_plan(plan)
   142→    print(colorize("  ✓ Reflect auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   143→    return True

   146→def _manual_clusters_or_error(plan: dict) -> list[str] | None:
   147→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   148→    if manual_clusters:
   149→        return manual_clusters
   150→    any_clusters = [
   151→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   152→    ]
   153→    if any_clusters:
   154→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   155→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   156→    else:
   157→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   158→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   159→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   160→    return None
   161→
