```
   238→def _shallow_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   239→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for clusters with steps lacking detail."""
   240→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   241→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   242→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   243→            continue
   244→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   245→        if not steps:
   246→            continue
   247→        bare = sum(
   248→            1 for s in steps
   249→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("detail") and not s.get("issue_refs")
   250→        )
   251→        if bare > 0:
   252→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
   253→    return results
   254→
   255→
   256→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   257→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
   258→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   259→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   260→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   261→            continue
   262→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   264→                continue
   265→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   266→            if not detail:
   267→                continue
   268→            bad: list[str] = []
   269→            for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(detail):
   270→                p = repo_root / path_str
   271→                if p.exists():
   272→                    continue
   273→                alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(p.suffix)
   274→                if alt_ext and p.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists():
   275→                    continue
   276→                bad.append(path_str)
   277→            if bad:
   278→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   279→    return results
   280→
   281→
   282→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   283→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   284→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   285→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   286→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   287→            continue
   288→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   289→        if not steps:
   290→            continue
   291→        missing = sum(
   292→            1 for s in steps
   293→            if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   294→        )
   295→        if missing:
   296→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   297→    return results
   298→
   299→
   300→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   301→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   302→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   303→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   304→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   305→            continue
   306→        paths: set[str] = set()
   307→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   308→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   309→                paths.update(_PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]))
   310→        if paths:
   311→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   312→
   313→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   314→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   315→    for i, a in enumerate(names):
   316→        for b in names[i + 1:]:
   317→            shared = cluster_files[a] & cluster_files[b]
   318→            if shared:
   319→                overlaps.append((a, b, sorted(shared)))
   320→    return overlaps
   321→
   322→
   323→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   324→    plan: dict, *, threshold: int = 5,
   325→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   326→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories.
   327→
   328→    A cluster with issues in 5+ distinct top-level directories is likely
   329→    grouped by theme/dimension rather than by file proximity.
   330→    Returns (cluster_name, dir_count, sample_dirs).
   331→    """
   332→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   333→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   334→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   335→            continue
   336→        # Collect file paths from step details
   337→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   338→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   339→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   340→                for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]):
   341→                    # Use first 2 path components as the "area"
   342→                    # e.g., src/domains/media-lightbox -> src/domains/media-lightbox
   343→                    parts = path_str.split("/")
   344→                    if len(parts) >= 3:
   345→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   346→                    elif len(parts) >= 2:
   347→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   348→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   349→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   350→    return results
   351→
   352→
   353→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   354→    plan: dict, *, max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   355→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   356→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping).
   357→
   358→    A well-organized cluster should consolidate related issues into fewer
   359→    steps (because multiple issues touching the same file become one step).
   360→    Returns (cluster_name, steps, issues, ratio).
   361→    """
   362→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   363→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   364→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   365→            continue
   366→        steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
   367→        issues = len(cluster.get("issue_ids") or [])
   368→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:  # only flag non-trivial clusters
   369→            ratio = steps / issues
   370→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   371→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   372→    return results
   373→
   374→
   375→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   376→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   377→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   378→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   379→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   380→            continue
   381→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   382→        if not steps:
   383→            continue
   384→        missing = sum(
   385→            1 for s in steps
   386→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("issue_refs")
   387→        )
   388→        if missing:
   389→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   390→    return results
   391→
   392→
   393→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   394→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready.
   395→
   396→    A step is "vague" if its detail is under 80 chars AND contains no file path.
   397→    Executor-ready means: someone with zero context can read the step and know
   398→    exactly which file to open and what to change.
   399→    """
   400→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   401→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   402→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   403→            continue
   404→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   405→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   406→                continue
   407→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   408→            if not detail:
   409→                continue
   410→            has_path = bool(_PATH_RE.search(detail))
   411→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   412→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   413→    return results
   414→
   415→
   416→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   417→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   418→    wontfixed = set()
   419→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   420→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   421→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   422→    # Also check the wontfix list directly
   423→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   424→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   425→
   426→    if not wontfixed:
   427→        return []
   428→
   429→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   430→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   431→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   432→            continue
   433→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   434→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   435→                continue
   436→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   437→            stale = [r for r in refs if r in wontfixed]
   438→            if stale:
   439→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   440→    return results
   441→
   442→
   443→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   444→    if not report:
   445→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   446→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   448→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   449→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   450→        return None
   451→    if len(report) < 100:
   452→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   453→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   454→        return None
   455→    return report
   456→
   457→
   458→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   459→    *,
   460→    plan: dict,
   461→    meta: dict,
   462→    stages: dict,
   463→) -> bool:
   464→    if "enrich" in stages:
   465→        return True
   466→    if "organize" not in stages:
   467→        # Fall through to existing organize requirement
   468→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   469→
   470→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   471→    if shallow:
   472→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
   473→        print(colorize(f"  {len(shallow)} cluster(s) have steps without detail or issue_refs:", "yellow"))
   474→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   475→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   476→        print(colorize(
   477→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   478→            "dim",
   479→        ))
   480→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   481→    else:
   482→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   483→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
   484→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   485→    return False
   486→
   487→
   488→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   489→    *,
   490→    plan: dict,
   491→    stages: dict,
   492→    attestation: str | None,
   493→) -> bool:
   494→    if stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   495→        return True
   496→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   497→        return False
   498→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   499→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   500→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   501→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.", "dim"))
   502→        return False
   503→
   504→    # Re-validate shallow steps at auto-confirm time
   505→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   506→    if shallow:
   507→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   508→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   509→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   510→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   511→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   512→        return False
   513→
   514→    cluster_names = [
   515→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   516→    ]
   517→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   518→        attestation.strip(),
   519→        "enrich",
   520→        cluster_names=cluster_names,
   521→    )
   522→    if validation_err:
   523→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   524→        return False
   525→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   526→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   527→    save_plan(plan)
   528→    print(colorize("  ✓ Enrich auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   529→    return True

   608→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
   609→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   610→    if not manual_clusters:

   612→            name

   614→            if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   615→        ]
   616→        if not any_clusters:

   618→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   619→            return False
   620→
   621→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   622→    if gaps:

   624→        for name, missing in gaps:

   626→        print(

   631→        )
   632→        print(
   633→            colorize(

   635→                "dim",

   637→        )
   638→        return False
   639→
   640→    # Check for unclustered review issues
   641→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   642→    if unclustered:

   644→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:

   649→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))

   651→
   652→    return True

   759→    *,

   766→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   767→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   768→    if confirmed:

   774→        )
   775→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   776→    return None

   779→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   780→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   781→    if not new_ids:
   782→        return True
   783→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   784→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   785→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   786→    if new_cited:
   787→        return True
   788→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   789→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   790→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   791→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   792→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   793→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   794→    return False
```

```
    11→def _print_observe_report_requirement() -> None:
    12→    print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
    13→    print(colorize("  Write an analysis of the issues: themes, root causes, contradictions.", "dim"))
    14→    print(colorize("  Identify issues that contradict each other (opposite recommendations).", "dim"))
    15→    print(colorize("  Do NOT just list issue IDs — describe what you actually observe.", "dim"))

    17→
    18→def _print_reflect_report_requirement() -> None:
    19→    print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage reflect.", "red"))
    20→    print(colorize("  Compare current issues against completed work and form a holistic strategy:", "dim"))
    21→    print(colorize("  - What clusters were previously completed? Did fixes hold?", "dim"))
    22→    print(colorize("  - Are any dimensions recurring (resolved before, open again)?", "dim"))
    23→    print(colorize("  - What contradictions did you find? Which direction will you take?", "dim"))
    24→    print(colorize("  - Big picture: what to prioritize, what to defer, what to skip?", "dim"))

    27→def _print_complete_summary(plan: dict, stages: dict) -> None:
    28→    print(colorize("  Triage summary:", "bold"))
    29→    if "observe" in stages:
    30→        observe_stage = stages["observe"]
    31→        print(colorize(f"    Observe: {observe_stage.get('issue_count', '?')} issues analysed", "dim"))
    32→    if "reflect" in stages:
    33→        reflect_stage = stages["reflect"]
    34→        recurring = reflect_stage.get("recurring_dims", [])
    35→        if recurring:
    36→            print(colorize(f"    Reflect: {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s)", "dim"))
    37→        else:
    38→            print(colorize("    Reflect: no recurring patterns", "dim"))
    39→    if "organize" not in stages:
    40→        return
    41→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"    Organize: {len(manual)} enriched cluster(s)", "dim"))
    43→    for name in manual:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
    45→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    46→        print(colorize(f"      {name}: {len(steps)} steps", "dim"))
    47→    if "enrich" in stages:
    48→        shallow = stages["enrich"].get("shallow_count", 0)
    49→        if shallow:
    50→            print(colorize(f"    Enrich: {shallow} step(s) still without detail", "dim"))
    51→        else:
    52→            print(colorize("    Enrich: all steps detailed", "dim"))

    59→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import _REVIEW_DETECTORS
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan import TRIAGE_IDS
     8→
     9→
    10→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    11→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    12→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    13→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    14→        return True
    15→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    16→    return False
    17→
    18→
    19→def _validate_stage_report(
    20→    report: str | None,
    21→    *,
    22→    stage: str,
    23→    min_chars: int,
    24→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    25→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> str | None:
    27→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    28→    if not report:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    30→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    31→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    32→        return None
    33→    cleaned = report.strip()
    34→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    38→                "red",
    39→            )
    40→        )
    41→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    42→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    43→        return None
    44→    return cleaned
    45→
    46→
    47→def _triage_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    48→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for triage progress."""
    49→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    52→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    53→    queue_ids = [issue_id for issue_id in plan.get("queue_order", []) if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    54→    organized = sum(1 for issue_id in queue_ids if issue_id in all_cluster_ids)
    55→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    56→
    57→
    58→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    59→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    60→
    61→    Requirements:
    62→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    63→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    64→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    65→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    66→    """
    67→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    68→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    69→        if not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    70→            continue
    71→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    72→            continue
    73→        missing: list[str] = []
    74→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    75→            missing.append("description")
    76→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    77→        issue_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    78→        if not steps:
    79→            missing.append("action_steps")
    80→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    81→            missing.append(
    82→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    83→            )
    84→        if missing:
    85→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    86→    return gaps
    87→
    88→
    89→def _unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    90→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    91→
    92→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    93→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    94→    compatibility.
    95→    """
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    98→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    99→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   100→            clustered_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   101→
   102→    if state is not None:
   103→        review_ids = [
   104→            fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   105→            if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   106→        ]
   107→    else:
   108→        review_ids = [
   109→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   110→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   111→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   112→        ]
   113→
   114→    return [fid for fid in review_ids if fid not in clustered_ids]
   115→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    12→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    13→    purge_ids,
    14→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    17→
    18→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    19→
    20→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]
    21→
    22→
    23→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    24→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    25→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    26→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    27→
    28→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    29→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    30→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    31→    existing = set(order)
    32→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    33→        if sid not in existing:
    34→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    35→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    36→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    37→        if sid in order:
    38→            order.remove(sid)
    39→    insert_at = 0
    40→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    41→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    42→        insert_at += 1
    43→
    44→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    45→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    46→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    47→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    48→
    49→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    51→    try:
    52→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    53→    except ValueError:
    54→        return []
    55→    cleared: list[str] = []
    56→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    57→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    60→            cleared.append(later)
    61→    return cleared
    62→
    63→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    64→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    65→    if not cleared:
    66→        return
    67→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    68→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    69→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    70→        None,
    71→    )
    72→    if next_unconfirmed:
    73→        print(colorize(
    74→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    75→            "dim",
    76→        ))
    77→
    78→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    79→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    80→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    81→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    82→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    83→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    84→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    85→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    86→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    87→
    88→def group_issues_into_observe_batches(
    89→    si,
    90→    max_batches: int = 5,
    91→) -> list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]]:
    92→    """Group issues by dimension into batches for parallel observe.
    93→
    94→    Returns list of (dimension_names, issues_subset) tuples.
    95→    Single batch if only one dimension exists.
    96→    """
    97→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    98→
    99→    if len(dim_names) <= 1:
   100→        return [(dim_names, dict(si.open_issues))]
   101→
   102→    # Distribute dimensions into balanced batches by issue count
   103→    num_batches = min(max_batches, len(dim_names))
   104→    batch_dims: list[list[str]] = [[] for _ in range(num_batches)]
   105→    batch_counts: list[int] = [0] * num_batches
   106→
   107→    # Greedy: assign each dimension (largest first) to the lightest batch
   108→    for dim in dim_names:
   109→        lightest = min(range(num_batches), key=lambda i: batch_counts[i])
   110→        batch_dims[lightest].append(dim)
   111→        batch_counts[lightest] += by_dim[dim]
   112→
   113→    # Build issue subsets per batch
   114→    # Pre-index issues by dimension
   115→    dim_to_issues: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = defaultdict(dict)
   116→    for fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
   117→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   119→        dim_to_issues[dim][fid] = f
   120→
   121→    result: list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]] = []
   122→    for dims in batch_dims:
   123→        if not dims:
   124→            continue
   125→        subset: dict[str, dict] = {}
   126→        for dim in dims:
   127→            subset.update(dim_to_issues.get(dim, {}))
   128→        if subset:
   129→            result.append((dims, subset))
   130→
   131→    return result
   132→
   133→
   134→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
   135→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
   136→    return {
   137→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   138→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   139→    }
   140→
   141→def triage_coverage(
   142→    plan: dict,
   143→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   144→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
   145→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
   146→
   147→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   148→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   149→    """
   150→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   151→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   152→    for c in clusters.values():
   153→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   154→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   155→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   156→    else:
   157→        review_ids = [
   158→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   159→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   160→        ]
   161→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   162→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   163→
   164→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   165→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   166→    return [
   167→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   168→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   169→    ]
   170→
   171→def apply_completion(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    plan: dict,
   174→    strategy: str,
   175→    *,
   176→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   177→) -> None:
   178→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   179→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   180→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   181→    state = runtime.state
   182→
   183→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(
   184→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   185→    )
   186→
   187→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   188→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   189→
   190→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   193→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   194→    open_ids = sorted(
   195→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   196→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   197→    )
   198→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_ids
   199→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   200→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   201→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   202→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   203→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   204→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   205→    if stages:
   206→        meta["last_triage"] = {
   207→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   208→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   209→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   210→        }
   211→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   212→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   213→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   214→
   215→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   216→
   217→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("issue_ids")])
   218→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   219→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   220→    if effective_strategy:
   221→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   222→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   223→
   224→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
   225→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
   226→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   227→        if fid in c.get("issue_ids", []):
   228→            return name
   229→    return None
   230→
   231→def count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   232→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
   233→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   234→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   235→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
   236→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
   237→    return dict(counts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "apply_completion",
   241→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   242→    "count_log_activity_since",
   243→    "find_cluster_for",
   244→    "group_issues_into_observe_batches",
   245→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   246→    "inject_triage_stages",
   247→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   248→    "observe_dimension_breakdown",
   249→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   250→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   251→    "purge_triage_stage",
   252→    "triage_coverage",
   253→]
   254→
```

```
1→"""Display and dashboard rendering for plan triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     6→from collections import defaultdict

    35→
    36→
    37→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    38→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
    39→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    40→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    41→        if stage_name in stages:
    42→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    43→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    44→            else:
    45→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    46→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    47→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    48→        else:
    49→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
    50→
    51→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
    52→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    53→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    54→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    55→        if not manual:
    56→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    57→        elif gaps:
    58→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    59→            for name, missing in gaps:
    60→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    61→            print(colorize(
    62→                f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
    63→                "dim",
    64→            ))
    65→        else:
    66→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    67→
    68→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    69→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    70→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    71→    # Only show clusters that actually have issues (hide empty/stale ones)
    72→    active_clusters = {
    73→        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
    74→        if c.get("issue_ids")
    75→    }
    76→    if active_clusters:
    77→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    78→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    79→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    80→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    81→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    82→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    83→            tags: list[str] = []
    84→            if auto:
    85→                tags.append("auto")
    86→            if desc:
    87→                tags.append("desc")
    88→            else:
    89→                tags.append("no desc")
    90→            if steps:
    91→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    92→            else:
    93→                if not auto:
    94→                    tags.append("no steps")
    95→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    96→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
    97→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
    98→
    99→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   100→    for c in clusters.values():
   101→        all_clustered.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   102→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
   103→    if unclustered:
   104→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   105→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   106→            f = open_issues[fid]
   107→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   108→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   109→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   110→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   111→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   112→    elif open_issues:
   113→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
   114→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
   115→
   116→def print_reflect_result(
   117→    *,
   118→    issue_count: int,
   119→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   120→    recurring: dict,
   121→    report: str,
   122→    is_reuse: bool,
   123→    cleared: list,
   124→    stages: dict,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Print the reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   127→    print(colorize(
   128→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, "
   129→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   130→        "green",
   131→    ))
   132→    if is_reuse:
   133→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   134→        if cleared:
   135→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   136→    else:
   137→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   138→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   139→    if recurring_dims:
   140→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   141→            info = recurring[dim]
   142→            print(colorize(
   143→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open",
   144→                "dim",
   145→            ))
   146→
   147→    print()
   148→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) \u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   149→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   150→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   151→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))
   152→    print_user_message(
   153→        "Reflect recorded. Before confirming — check the"
   154→        " subagent's report. Is it a strategy or just observe"
   155→        " restated? It should include a concrete cluster"
   156→        " blueprint: which clusters, which issues, what to skip"
   157→        " (with per-issue reasons). Confirm when the blueprint"
   158→        " is specific enough for organize to execute mechanically."
   159→    )
   160→
   161→def print_organize_result(
   162→    *,
   163→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   164→    plan: dict,
   165→    report: str,
   166→    is_reuse: bool,
   167→    cleared: list,
   168→    stages: dict,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    """Print the organize stage output including cluster summary and next steps."""
   171→    print(colorize(
   172→        f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
   173→        "green",
   174→    ))
   175→    if is_reuse:
   176→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   177→        if cleared:
   178→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   179→    else:
   180→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   181→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   182→    for name in manual_clusters:
   183→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   184→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   185→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   186→        desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   187→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('issue_ids', []))} issues, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   188→
   189→    print()
   190→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Prioritized organization (share with user) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   191→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   192→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   193→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))
   194→    print_user_message(
   195→        "Organize recorded. Before confirming — does the"
   196→        " organize output match the reflect blueprint? Clusters"
   197→        " by file area (same PR), not by theme? Step count <"
   198→        " issue count (consolidated)? Cluster names describe"
   199→        " locations, not problem types? This should read like"
   200→        " a set of PR plans."
   201→    )
   202→
   203→def print_reflect_dashboard(
   204→    si: object,
   205→    plan: dict,
   206→    *,
   207→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   208→) -> None:
   209→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
   210→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   211→    # si is a TriageInput
   212→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   213→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
   214→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
   215→
   216→    if completed:
   217→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   218→        for c in completed[:10]:
   219→            name = c.get("name", "?")
   220→            count = len(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   221→            thesis = c.get("thesis", "")
   222→            print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
   223→            if thesis:
   224→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   225→            for step in c.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   226→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   227→        if len(completed) > 10:
   228→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   229→
   230→    if resolved:
   231→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   232→        for fid, f in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   233→            status = f.get("status", "")
   234→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   235→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   236→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   237→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   238→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   239→        if len(resolved) > 10:
   240→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   241→
   242→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved)
   243→    if recurring:
   244→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   245→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   246→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   247→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   248→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   249→            print(colorize(
   250→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   251→                "yellow",
   252→            ))
   253→    elif not completed and not resolved:
   254→        print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   255→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   256→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   257→        print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   258→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   259→
   260→def _print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   261→    """Print the header section: title, open issues count, stage progress, overall status."""
   262→    print(colorize("  Epic triage \u2014 manual", "bold"))
   263→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   264→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   265→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   266→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   267→    if si.existing_epics:
   268→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   269→    if si.new_since_last:
   270→        print(colorize(f"  New since last triage: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
   271→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   272→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   273→            dim = ""
   274→            detail = f.get("detail")
   275→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
   276→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   277→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
   278→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
   279→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   280→        print(f"  Resolved since last triage: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   281→
   282→    # Stage progress (with enrichment gaps)
   283→    print()
   284→    print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
   285→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
   286→        print(
   287→            colorize(
   288→                "  Note: review issues changed since stage progress started; "
   289→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
   290→                "yellow",
   291→            )
   292→        )
   293→
   294→
   295→def _print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   296→    """Print the 'What to do' action guidance section based on current stage."""
   297→    print()
   298→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   299→    if "observe" not in stages and has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   300→        # Show both paths: accept or re-plan
   301→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   302→        print()
   303→        print(colorize("  To accept current queue (new items at end):", "cyan"))
   304→        print(
   305→            '    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing '
   306→            '--note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."'
   307→        )
   308→        print()
   309→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   310→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   311→    elif "observe" not in stages:
   312→        print(colorize("  Next step:", "yellow"))
   313→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   314→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   315→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   316→        print(colorize("  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.", "yellow"))
   317→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT}")
   318→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   319→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   320→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   321→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   322→
   323→        if not manual:
   324→            print(colorize("  Next steps:", "yellow"))
   325→            print("    0. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   326→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   327→            print(f"    2. Add issues:     {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   328→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")

   330→        elif gaps:

   332→            for name, missing in gaps:

   334→            print(colorize(

   336→                "dim",
   337→            ))
   338→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   339→        else:
   340→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   341→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   342→
   343→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   344→            print()
   345→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track (if existing plan is still valid):", "dim"))
   346→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   347→    elif "enrich" not in stages:
   348→        shallow = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   349→        if shallow:
   350→            print(colorize("  Next step: enrich steps with detail and issue_refs.", "yellow"))
   351→            print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   352→        else:
   353→            print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the enrich stage:", "green"))
   354→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH}")
   355→        print(colorize("  You can still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder items.", "dim"))
   356→    else:
   357→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   358→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE}")
   359→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   360→
   361→
   362→def _print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   363→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   364→    if "observe" in stages:
   365→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   366→        if obs_report:
   367→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   368→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   369→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   370→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   371→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   372→    if "reflect" in stages:
   373→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   374→        if ref_report:
   375→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   376→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   377→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   378→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   379→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   380→
   381→
   382→def _print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   383→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   384→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   385→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
   386→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   387→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   388→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, f))
   389→
   390→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   391→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   392→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   393→    max_per_dim = 5
   394→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   395→        items = by_dim[dim]
   396→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   397→        for fid, f in items[:max_per_dim]:
   398→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   399→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   400→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   401→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   402→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   403→            if suggestion:
   404→                print(colorize(f"        \u2192 {suggestion}", "dim"))
   405→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   406→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   407→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   408→
   409→
   410→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   411→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   412→    *,
   413→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   414→) -> None:
   415→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   416→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   417→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   418→    state = runtime.state
   419→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   420→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   421→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   422→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   423→
   424→    _print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   425→    _print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   426→    _print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   427→    _print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   428→
   429→    # Show reflect dashboard when observe done, reflect not done
   430→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   431→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   432→
   433→    # Show current cluster progress
   434→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   435→
   436→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   437→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   438→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   439→    active = {
   440→        n: c for n, c in clusters.items()
   441→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   442→    }
   443→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   444→
   445→    if active:
   446→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   447→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   448→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   449→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   450→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   451→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   452→
   453→    queue_order = [
   454→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   455→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   456→    ]
   457→    if queue_order:
   458→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   459→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   460→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   461→            f = issues.get(fid, {})
   462→            summary = (f.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   463→            detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   464→            cn = find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   465→            print(f"    {i+1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cn})' if cn else ''}")
   466→
   467→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(
   468→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   469→    )
   470→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   471→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "cmd_triage_dashboard",
   475→    "print_organize_result",
   476→    "print_progress",
   477→    "print_reflect_dashboard",
   478→    "print_reflect_result",
   479→    "print_stage_progress",
   480→    "show_plan_summary",
   481→]
   482→
```

```
    34→def _parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    36→    if not raw:
    37→        return list(_STAGES)
    38→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    39→    for s in stages:
    40→        if s not in _STAGES:
    41→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {s!r}. Valid: {', '.join(_STAGES)}")
    42→    return stages

    50→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):

    52→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    53→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    54→        services.save_plan(plan)
    55→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started.", "cyan"))
    56→    return plan

   110→def _merge_observe_outputs(
   111→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
   112→) -> str:
   113→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
   114→    parts: list[str] = []
   115→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
   116→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
   117→        content = ""
   118→        if output_file.exists():
   119→            try:
   120→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   121→            except OSError:
   122→                content = "(batch output missing)"
   123→        if not content:
   124→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
   125→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
   126→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   127→
   128→
   129→def _run_observe(
   130→    *,
   131→    si,
   132→    repo_root: Path,
   133→    prompts_dir: Path,
   134→    output_dir: Path,
   135→    logs_dir: Path,
   136→    timeout_seconds: int,
   137→    dry_run: bool = False,
   138→    append_run_log=None,
   139→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   140→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches.
   141→
   142→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success, False on failure.
   143→    """
   144→    from functools import partial
   145→
   146→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   147→
   148→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   149→
   150→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   151→
   152→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
   153→    total = len(batches)
   154→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
   155→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
   156→
   157→    # Build prompts and tasks
   158→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   159→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
   160→
   161→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
   162→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
   163→            batch_index=i + 1,
   164→            total_batches=total,
   165→            dimension_group=dims,
   166→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
   167→            repo_root=repo_root,
   168→        )
   169→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
   170→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   171→
   172→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
   173→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
   174→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
   175→
   176→        if not dry_run:
   177→            tasks[i] = partial(
   178→                run_triage_stage,
   179→                prompt=prompt,
   180→                repo_root=repo_root,
   181→                output_file=output_file,
   182→                log_file=log_file,
   183→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   184→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   185→            )
   186→
   187→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
   188→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
   189→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
   190→
   191→    if dry_run:
   192→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   193→        return True, ""
   194→
   195→    # Progress callback — same pattern as review batch execution
   196→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   197→        idx = event.batch_index
   198→        if event.event == "start":
   199→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} started", "dim"))
   200→            _log(f"observe-batch-start batch={idx + 1}")
   201→        elif event.event == "done":
   202→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   203→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   204→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   205→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   206→            _log(f"observe-batch-done batch={idx + 1} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   207→        elif event.event == "heartbeat":
   208→            details = event.details or {}
   209→            active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   210→            elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   211→            if active:
   212→                parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   213→                print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   214→
   215→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   216→        _log(f"observe-batch-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   217→
   218→    # Execute in parallel
   219→    failures = execute_batches(
   220→        tasks=tasks,
   221→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   222→        progress_fn=_progress,
   223→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   224→    )
   225→
   226→    if failures:
   227→        print(colorize(
   228→            f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   229→            "red",
   230→        ))
   231→        for idx in failures:
   232→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   233→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   234→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   235→        return False, ""
   236→
   237→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   238→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   239→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   240→    return True, merged
   241→
   242→
   243→def _run_codex_pipeline(
   244→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   245→    *,
   246→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   247→) -> None:
   248→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   249→    from .codex_runner import run_triage_stage
   250→
   251→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   252→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   253→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   254→
   255→    try:
   256→        stages_to_run = _parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
   257→    except ValueError as exc:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   259→        return
   260→
   261→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   262→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   263→    _ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
   264→
   265→    # Create run directory
   266→    stamp = _run_stamp()
   267→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   268→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   269→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   270→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   271→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   272→    for d in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   273→        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   274→
   275→    # Run log — same pattern as review batch execution
   276→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   277→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   278→    append_run_log(
   279→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   280→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   281→    )
   282→
   283→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   284→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   285→
   286→    # Collect triage input
   287→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   288→    state = runtime.state
   289→
   290→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   291→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   292→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   293→
   294→    for stage in stages_to_run:
   295→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   296→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   297→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   298→
   299→        # Skip if already confirmed (resume support)
   300→        if stage in stages and stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   301→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   302→            append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   303→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   304→            report = stages[stage].get("report", "")
   305→            if report:
   306→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   307→            continue
   308→
   309→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   310→        append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   311→
   312→        # Collect fresh triage input each stage
   313→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   314→
   315→        # Parallel observe path
   316→        used_parallel = False
   317→        if stage == "observe":
   318→            parallel_ok, merged_report = _run_observe_parallel(
   319→                si=si,
   320→                repo_root=repo_root,
   321→                prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   322→                output_dir=output_dir,
   323→                logs_dir=logs_dir,
   324→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   325→                dry_run=dry_run,
   326→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   327→            )
   328→            if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   329→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   330→                continue
   331→            if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   332→                # Record observe via the stage command with merged report
   333→                from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_observe
   334→                record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   335→                    stage="observe",
   336→                    report=merged_report,
   337→                    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   338→                )
   339→                cmd_stage_observe(record_args, services=resolved_services)
   340→                used_parallel = True
   341→            elif parallel_ok is False:
   342→                # Parallel execution failed — abort pipeline
   343→                elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   344→                print(colorize("  Observe: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   345→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage=observe elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed")
   346→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   347→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   348→                return
   349→            # parallel_ok is None → not applicable, fall through to single path
   350→
   351→        # Single-subprocess path (non-observe stages, or observe with too few issues)
   352→        if not used_parallel:
   353→            prompt = build_stage_prompt(
   354→                stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root,
   355→            )
   356→
   357→            prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   358→            safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   359→
   360→            if dry_run:
   361→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   362→                print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   363→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   364→                continue
   365→
   366→            print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   367→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   368→
   369→            output_file = output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   370→            log_file = logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   371→
   372→            exit_code = run_triage_stage(
   373→                prompt=prompt,
   374→                repo_root=repo_root,
   375→                output_file=output_file,
   376→                log_file=log_file,
   377→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   378→            )
   379→
   380→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   381→            append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-done stage={stage} code={exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   382→
   383→            if exit_code != 0:
   384→                print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {exit_code}).", "red"))
   385→                print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   386→                print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   387→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={exit_code}")
   388→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "exit_code": exit_code, "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   389→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   390→                return
   391→
   392→        # Common path: reload for validation
   393→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   394→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   395→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   396→
   397→        # Validate stage
   398→        ok, error_msg = validate_stage(stage, plan, state, repo_root, triage_input=si)
   399→        if not ok:
   400→            elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   401→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
   402→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   403→            append_run_log(f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}")
   404→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "validation_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed, "error": error_msg}
   405→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        # Auto-confirm via confirmation functions with generated attestation
   409→        attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, si)
   410→
   411→        # Build a synthetic args namespace for confirm functions
   412→        confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
   413→            confirm=stage,
   414→            attestation=attestation,
   415→            state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   416→        )
   417→
   418→        from ..confirmations import _cmd_confirm_stage
   419→        _cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=resolved_services)
   420→
   421→        # Re-load and check confirmation took effect
   422→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   423→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   424→        stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   425→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   426→        if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   427→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   428→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   429→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   430→        else:
   431→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   432→            print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   433→            append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   434→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   435→            _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   436→            return
   437→
   438→        # Accumulate report for next stage's prompt
   439→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   440→        if report:
   441→            prior_reports[stage] = report
   442→
   443→    if dry_run:
   444→        print(colorize("\n  [dry-run] All prompts generated. No stages executed.", "cyan"))
   445→        _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    # Auto-complete triage
   449→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   450→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   451→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   452→
   453→    # Derive strategy from all stage reports
   454→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   455→    for s in _STAGES:
   456→        report = stages_data.get(s, {}).get("report", "")
   457→        if report:
   458→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{s}] {report[:200]}")
   459→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   460→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   461→        strategy = strategy + " " + "Automated triage via codex subagent pipeline. " * 3
   462→
   463→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   464→
   465→    # Build attestation for completion
   466→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("enrich", plan, si)
   467→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   468→        complete=True,
   469→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   470→        attestation=attestation,
   471→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   472→    )
   473→
   474→    from ..stage_completion_commands import _cmd_triage_complete
   475→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=resolved_services)
   476→
   477→    total_elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - pipeline_start)
   478→    print(colorize(f"\n  Triage pipeline complete ({total_elapsed}s).", "green"))
   479→    append_run_log(f"run-finished elapsed={total_elapsed}s")
   480→    _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   481→
   482→
   483→def _write_triage_run_summary(
   484→    run_dir: Path,
   485→    stamp: str,
   486→    stages: list[str],
   487→    stage_results: dict[str, dict],
   488→    append_run_log,
   489→) -> None:
   490→    """Write a run_summary.json with per-stage results."""
   491→    summary = {
   492→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   493→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   494→        "runner": "codex",
   495→        "stages_requested": stages,
   496→        "stage_results": stage_results,
   497→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   498→    }
   499→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   500→    safe_write_text(summary_path, json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   501→    print(colorize(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   502→    append_run_log(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = ["do_run_triage_stages"]
   506→
```
